| Subject | Section | `hp0` | `dp` | To FAILED | To DESTROYED (`T_destroy`) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `p_cryo_rad` (CLT-LEAK, live) | HOME | 3800 | 16 | 50 periods = 8.3 min | 238 periods = 39.7 min |
| `d_cryo_exposed` (O2-SHIP, behind the closed `v_o2_cryo`) | HOME | 2000 | 16 | already FAILED | **125 periods = 20.8 min from wake** |
| `pf_feed` (FUEL-DRIP, live) | HOME | 3999 | 16 | 63 periods = 10.5 min | 250 periods = 41.7 min |
| `pa_frozen` (dead, forced FREEZE) | DERELICT | 2500 | 32 | already FAILED | not applicable while forced FREEZE (not in mode LEAK) `[UNV: Graph]`; if thawed, 79 periods = 13.2 min |
| `pb_leak` (dead) | DERELICT | 2900 | 32 | already FAILED | **91 periods = 15.2 min** of B-loaded time |

The `d_cryo_exposed` row is the clock the closed-valve staging hides (no hiss, no trail, no glow; ASK-G1, DA-16). Three branches, none of which this GDD resolves: **(a) literal** (current): the table above is the content clock, `G0_ZERO_PART_SURVIVABLE` includes the "`v_o2_cryo` never opened" case and the closed-valve staging cannot pass it; **(b) gated** (ASK-G1 accepted: wear only while `intensity_pm` > 0): a closed valve holds `hp`, the `d_cryo_exposed` clock starts when the valve opens, dead leaky faults are stable, and `pb_leak`'s 15.2-minute window disappears; **(c) fallback** (the Graph declines, or has not answered by MVP content lock; DA-16's trigger): `v_o2_cryo` OPEN at wake, the hiss is the tell, the served `scr_main` holds the pool, and the D.19 ship clock begins only when the scrubber loses power. *Gate caveat `[UNV: Graph]`:* LSG D.9 floors a FAILED subject at `hp` 1 while the D.11 fairness gate is closed, and that gate opens only after a `warn_since_step` above 0 with `sev` at least 1 for 100 steps; whether an authored-at-build subject carries a set `warn_since_step` is not stated, so the DESTROYED figures above are the **worst case** (the node is gone and `NOT_REPAIRABLE`), and the milder case (held at `hp` 1) is what `DEAD_FAULT_STILL_REPAIRABLE` measures (DA-42; the question joins ASK-G1 as OQ-DA-21).

### D.15 Patch and fatigue treadmills

The `patch_fatigue_treadmill` formula is defined as:

`d_decay = floor(PATCH_DECAY_BP_PER_100 x rate_pm / 1000)` ; `n_dec = ceil((PATCH_CAP_BP - (LEAK_HP - 1)) / d_decay)` ; `T_cycle_steps = PATCH_DECAY_STEPS + n_dec x WEAR_PERIOD_STEPS` ; `d_fat = floor(HULL_FATIGUE_BP_PER_100 x rate_pm / 1000)` ; `n_hold = PATCH_DECAY_STEPS / WEAR_PERIOD_STEPS` (24 periods) ; `n_fat = ceil((PATCH_CAP_BP - (BREACH_HP - 1)) / d_fat)` if that value is at most `n_hold`, else `n_hold + ceil((PATCH_CAP_BP - n_hold x d_fat - (BREACH_HP - 1)) / (d_fat + d_decay))`

**Variables:**
| Variable | Type | Range | Source | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `PATCH_CAP_BP` | int bp | 6000 (range 4500 to 6900) | registry | `hp` ceiling of a PATCH (M7) |
| `LEAK_HP`, `BREACH_HP` | int bp | 4000, 4000 | registry (Graph-owned); LSG:102, LS-11 | Re-leak and re-breach thresholds |
| `PATCH_DECAY_BP_PER_100`, `PATCH_DECAY_STEPS` | int | 100 / 2400 | LSG D.9 | Patch hold (4 minutes) then decay per period |
| `HULL_FATIGUE_BP_PER_100` | int | 50 | LSG D.9 | Fatigue wear |
| `rate_pm` | int permille | 1000 / 2000 | D.14 | HOME or DERELICT |

**Output Range:** `T_cycle_steps` 3,500 to 4,500 (5.8 to 7.5 minutes); `n_fat` 21 to 30 periods (3.5 minutes on a DERELICT; 5.0 minutes on HOME, where the decay term joins after the 24-period hold).
**Example:** a leaky HOME fault: `d_decay` 100, `n_dec` ceil(2001 / 100) = 21, `T_cycle` 2400 + 2100 = 4,500 steps = **7.5 minutes**, 8.0 patches per hour; on a DERELICT `d_decay` 200, `n_dec` 11, `T_cycle` 3,500 steps = **5.8 minutes**, 10.3 per hour. `seg_b_pump_n` (B is a DERELICT-kind section even after the weld): `d_fat` 100, `n_fat` ceil(2001 / 100) = 21 periods = 210 s = **3.5 minutes** (under the 24-period hold, so decay never adds); the HOME figure for comparison is `d_fat` 50, 41 periods without decay, 30 periods = 5.0 minutes with it. G-0 workload: the three leaky home faults (CLT-LEAK, O2-SHIP, FUEL-DRIP) need 24 patches per hour, each about 340 ticks (a `PATCH_CHANNEL_TICKS` 180 `[PLACEHOLDER: Interaction]` channel plus a 160-tick run of 480 px), 24 x 340 / 60 = 136 s, 2.3 minutes per hour (3.8 %): a heartbeat, not a siege. `[UNV: Interaction]` one channel is assumed to restore `hp` to `PATCH_CAP_BP`, and `[UNV: Graph]` a PATCH on a fatigued segment at `hp` 6999 (above the cap) gains nothing, so only a FULL repair stops the fatigue (EC below).

### D.16 Derelict A bus on its battery (the pump-stall window)

The `bus_on_battery_window` formula is defined as:

`t_bus_steps = floor(C_bus / R_served)` ; `R_served = sum of requests served on the bus` ; `PUMP_TELL_OK iff t_bus_steps >= PUMP_TELL_MIN_STEPS` ; `PUMP_TELL_FLOOR_STEPS = W_MIN_WARNING_S x TICK_RATE / 6` (= 100 steps = 10 s) ; `PUMP_TELL_FAIRNESS_OK iff t_bus_steps >= PUMP_TELL_FLOOR_STEPS` (the CI lint `PUMP_TELL_UNDER_FAIRNESS`, ERROR, on the conservative `R_served`; the 900-step target is a design intent reported beside it, so P2 is protected by the build and not by the arithmetic of this example)

**Variables:**
| Variable | Type | Range | Source | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `C_bus` | int dJ | 180,000 to 1,440,000 | authored `b_a2` `stock` (DA-19) | Stock of the battery that feeds A's bus |
| `R_served` | int W = dJ per step | 20 to 1600 | LSG D.6 tiers; DA-20 | Scrubber `scr_a` 600, pump `pump_a` 400, `htr_a` 500, five bus lamps 100 (the residual lamp `lamp_a_life` is on its own island) |
| `PUMP_TELL_MIN_STEPS` | int steps | 900 (90 s) | Authoring knob | Service time the pump-stall tell needs to be seen (the design target; reported beside the computed figure, WARN `PUMP_TELL_BELOW_TARGET` when under) |
| `PUMP_TELL_FLOOR_STEPS` | int steps | 100 (10 s) | `W_MIN_WARNING_S` 10 s (registry) x `TICK_RATE` 60 / 6 ticks per Graph step | The fairness floor; below it `PUMP_TELL_UNDER_FAIRNESS` is an ERROR (AC-DA-134) |

**Output Range:** 112 steps (the smallest cell, full bus) to 27,000 (the residual lamp alone, D.6).
**Example:** with the junction swapped and the tie closed, `b_a2` at 1,440,000 dJ and the **full bus** drawing 1,600 W: floor(1,440,000 / 1600) = **900 steps = 90.0 s**, exactly the design target, **zero margin against the target** but **800 steps (80 s) above the 100-step fairness floor**, which the CI lint holds even if an upstream placeholder (battery charge, pump or scrubber draw) drifts (carried into the Tuning Knobs row). Shedding `scr_a` (the player cuts it for the carry, V8) leaves 1,000 W: 1,440 steps = 144 s. The pump alone: 3,600 steps = 6.0 minutes. Assumptions: whether a FAILED `scr_a` still requests its 600 W, the battery discharge cap and tier shedding order are `[UNV: Graph]`; A has no reactor, so `b_a2` only drains: the bus is a clock, not a base. The earlier `b_a1`-only arrangement (540,000 dJ) gives 337 steps = 33.7 s on the full bus, too short for the stall to be read, which is why `b_a2` exists.

### D.17 Reachable-set subject budget

The `subject_budget_per_set` formula is defined as:

`S_int(S) = F(S) + L_seed(S) + C` ; `S_total(S) = S_int(S) + PLAYER_DROP_RESERVE + ACTORS + HORRORS` ; `SUBJECT_BUDGET_SET iff S_int(S) > INTERACT_FIXTURE_BUDGET or S_total(S) > MAX_EXPOSURE_SUBJECTS` ; `I-7: INTERACT_FIXTURE_BUDGET + PLAYER_DROP_RESERVE <= floor(3 x MAX_EXPOSURE_SUBJECTS / 4)` ; `MAX_EXPOSURE_SUBJECTS_needed = ceil(4 x (INTERACT_FIXTURE_BUDGET + PLAYER_DROP_RESERVE) / 3)`

**Variables:**
| Variable | Type | Range | Source | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `F` | int | 0 to 120 | IA D.10 and IA-37; declared interactables (hardpoints, two face subjects per door, valves, hull segments, sockets) | Home 48, A 28, B 40 |
| `L_seed` | int | 0 to 20 | DA-26 | Seeded loose placements still on the floor: home 2, A 4, B 4 (falls as the player collects) |
| `C` | int | 0 to 18 | IA D.10 | Cap-stub worst case (at most 6 modules x 3 ports), counted once per set |
| `PLAYER_DROP_RESERVE`, `ACTORS`, `HORRORS` (Interaction's `P`, `A`, `H`) | int | 24, 4, 2 | IA D.10, I-7; registry `subject_budget` | 30 together; `H` is `HORROR_SUBJECTS_MAX` 2 `[PLACEHOLDER: Horror]` Emitter HORROR subjects (one active plus one transient) |
| `INTERACT_FIXTURE_BUDGET` | int | 120 `[PLACEHOLDER: Interaction]` | registry (Interaction-owned, IA-37) | Content ceiling for `S_int` |
| `MAX_EXPOSURE_SUBJECTS` | int | 192 `[PLACEHOLDER: Emitter]` (registry range 128 to 384) | registry (Emitter-owned) | Subject cap |

**Output Range:** `S_int` 68 to 144 and `S_total` 98 to 174 in MVP content.
**Example:**

| Set | `F` | `L_seed` | `C` | `S_int` | vs 120 | `S_total` | vs 192 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| S0 `{home}` | 48 | 2 | 18 | 68 | pass | 98 | pass |
| S1 `{home, A}` | 76 | 6 | 18 | 100 | pass | 130 | pass |
| S2, S3 `{home, B}` / `{home+B}` | 88 | 6 | 18 | 112 | pass (8 slack) | 142 | pass |
| **S4 `{home+B, A}`** | 116 | 10 | 18 | **144** | **over by 24** | 174 | pass (18 slack) |

I-7 check: 120 + 24 = 144 = floor(3 x 192 / 4) with `MAX_EXPOSURE_SUBJECTS` 192 `[PLACEHOLDER: Emitter]`, exactly at the limit. Raising the budget to 144 alone breaks I-7 (144 + 24 = 168 > 144) and needs `MAX_EXPOSURE_SUBJECTS` of ceil(4 x 168 / 3) = **224** (a two-owner change, Checkpoint rulings Q4 option 2). The Authoring-only diet (option 3) must remove at least 24 subjects from S4 to bring `S_int` to 120 (the Edge Cases list the named candidates and their readability cost).

### D.18 Thermal drift (the cold-room invariant)

The `thermal_drift` formula is defined as (a lint and report formula, harness-side; the natural logarithm is forbidden on the sim path and nothing hashed depends on it):

`tau_steps = floor(100 x V x C_TILE / (K_HULL x S_ext))` = `floor(12000 x V / S_ext)` at the defaults ; `t_cross_min = tau_steps x ln((T0 - T_AMB_MC) / (ONSET_MC - T_AMB_MC)) / 600` for `T0 > ONSET_MC` ; `COLD_ROOM_DRIFT iff t_cross_min < COLD_ROOM_SAFE_MIN` (sealed, unheated, doors closed) ; `COLD_ROOM_UNDECLARED iff T0 <= ONSET_MC and the room is not the declared lethal room`

**Variables:**
| Variable | Type | Range | Source | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `V` | int tiles | 4 to 200 | LY D.3 | Room volume |
| `S_ext` | int tiles | 1 to 80 | DA-7 `exposed_tiles` (`THERMAL_INPUT_MISSING` if absent) | Hull tiles exposed |
| `C_TILE`, `K_HULL` | int | 3000 / 25 | LSG D.7 | Thermal mass per tile (dJ per C) and hull conductance |
| `T0` | int mC | authored | C.1 to C.3 | Initial room temperature |
| `T_AMB_MC` | int mC | -30000 | LSG (R14; MVP exterior sink) | Exterior temperature |
| `ONSET_MC` | int mC | -20000 | SE D.4 (`COLD_DMG_ONSET_MC` -15000 less `INSUL_MC` 5000 `[PLACEHOLDER: Suit]`) | Shifted cold onset, at full suit integrity (at or above `SUIT_LEAK_HP`); a leaking suit moves the onset toward `COLD_DMG_ONSET_MC` -15000 (SE-21), and `COLD_ROOM_DRIFT` also prints the -15000 projection |
| `COLD_ROOM_SAFE_MIN` | int min | 25 (range 25 to 60) | Authoring knob (Checkpoint rulings Q7; not registry) | Required loaded minutes above the onset |

**Output Range:** 0 to unbounded minutes; the room never falls below `T_AMB_MC` + ceil(100000 / (`K_HULL` x `S_ext`)) (the plate-term stall).
**Example (design values, doors closed):**

| Room | `V` | `S_ext` | `T0` (mC) | `tau_steps` | `t_cross_min` |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `a_dock` | 20 | 13 | -6000 | 18,461 | **26.9** |
| `a_spine` | 33 | 11 | -12000 | 36,000 | 35.3 |
| `a_life` | 30 | 11 | -10000 | 32,727 | 37.8 |
| `a_machine` | 35 | 13 | -22000 | 32,307 | below the onset: declared lethal (D.10) |
| `b_dock` | 20 | 13 | 22000 | 18,461 | 50.7 |
| `b_spine` | 39 | 16 | 30000 | 29,250 | 87.3 |
| `b_reactor` | 40 | 21 | 42000 | 22,857 | 75.2 (cold-drift form only; the post-V12 heat projection is the paragraph below) |
| `b_pump` | 20 | 8 | 38000 | 30,000 | 95.8 |
| `b_breach` | 30 | 17 | 10000 | 21,176 | 48.9 (vacuum; reported, not a conduction case) |

`a_dock` is the tightest room at 26.9 minutes against 25 (margin 1.9); at the -15000 projection of a leaking suit it falls to 14.5 minutes (`a_spine` 10.9), printed and reviewed, not a lint threshold. With a door open, `K_OPEN` conduction mixes `a_spine` toward `a_machine`, and the figure falls: the lint prints the open-door projection beside every closed-door row. `a_spine` at -12000 is below `FREEZE_WARN_MC` -10000, so any PIPE there would carry a permanent FREEZE pre-stage warning; A's corridor is authored with no pipes for that reason.

**Warm-up of a heated room (the cryo bay after V3, DA-45; harness-side, derived `[derived]`).** `gross_mC_per_step = floor(1000 x P_dJ / (V x C_TILE))` with `P_dJ` = 500 W x 0.1 s x 10 = 500 dJ per Graph step ; `q_hull_dJ = K_HULL x S_ext x |T - T_AMB_MC| / 100000` ; `net_mC_per_step = floor(1000 x (P_dJ - q_hull_dJ) / (V x C_TILE))` ; `warm_steps = ceil((T_COMFORT_MC - T0) / mean net)`. For `cryo_bay` (`V` 42, `S_ext` 13): C = 42 x 3000 = 126,000 dJ per degree C, gross 500 x 1000 / 126,000 = 3.97 mC per step (about 4); `q_hull` at -2000 mC is 25 x 13 x 28,000 / 100,000 = 91 dJ (net 3.25 mC) and at 10000 mC is 25 x 13 x 40,000 / 100,000 = 130 dJ (net 2.94 mC), mean about 3.1; from -2000 to the COMFORT floor 10000 mC (LSG:96) is 12,000 mC, so about 3,900 Graph steps = 390 s = **about 6.5 minutes**. LSG:839's 2.5 minutes (500 x 1000 / 60,000 = 8.3 mC per step) is the Graph's example for a 20-tile room (60,000 = 20 x `C_TILE`), not this 42-tile bay. Routed to the Graph owner: LSG:839's figure assumes V 20 (no value changes). Authoring keeps the slower climb (Game Feel) rather than retune the verbatim fixture; the only lever, a second heater, is the PWR-OVL trigger (`CRYO_HEATER_COUNT`, Tuning Knobs).

**Heat projection after a reactor restart (`b_reactor`, DA-28; `[derived; Graph owner to confirm Q_src_room]`).** `T_eq_hot = T_AMB_MC + ceil(Q_src_room x 100000 / (K_HULL x S_ext))` ; `t_onset_steps = ceil(tau_steps x ln((T_eq_hot - T0) / (T_eq_hot - HEAT_DMG_ONSET_MC)))` when `T_eq_hot` > `HEAT_DMG_ONSET_MC`, else never ; `e = T_eq_hot - HEAT_DMG_ONSET_MC` feeds D.10 with Suit's heat gain. For `b_reactor` (`S_ext` 21, `tau_steps` 22,857, `Q_src_room` 516 dJ per step, the Graph's D.8 worked example `[UNV: Graph]`): `T_eq_hot` = -30000 + ceil(51,600,000 / 525) = -30000 + 98,286 = **68,286 mC**; `t_onset_steps` = ceil(22,857 x ln(26,286 / 23,286)) = 2,770 steps (**4.6 minutes**); `e` 23,286 is above `TL_DEPTH_CAP_MC` 20,000, so `gain` 40, `runs_critical` ceil(4000 / 40) = 100 (10.0 s) and `runs_failed` 300 (30.0 s). A `T0` of 45000 would put the room on the onset from step 0 and damaging with every uncaptured dJ, which is why the authored value sits 3000 mC under it. **Tell lead (the presentation step before the onset; `[derived]`).** `t_tell_steps = ceil(tau_steps x ln((T_eq_hot - T0) / (T_eq_hot - HEAT_TELL_STEP_MC)))` ; `lead_steps = t_onset_steps - t_tell_steps` ; `HEAT_TELL_LEAD_OK iff lead_steps >= W_MIN_WARNING_S x STEPS_PER_S` with `STEPS_PER_S` = `TICK_RATE` / 6 = 10 (Graph hook divisor 6; D.15's 2400 steps = 4 minutes). For `b_reactor` with `HEAT_TELL_STEP_MC` 44,700: `t_tell_steps` = ceil(22,857 x ln(26,286 / 23,586)) = 2,477 (4.1 minutes after V12), `lead_steps` = 2,770 - 2,477 = **293 (29.3 s, 1,758 ticks)**, above the 100-step floor; the step may be set no higher than 44,897 mC (lead 100) and the authored value leaves a margin of 193 steps. If `T_eq_hot` is at most `HEAT_DMG_ONSET_MC` the room never damages and the lead is not required.

### D.19 Cryo-bay and ship air clocks

The `air_clock` formula is defined as:

`t_band_steps = ceil((P0_DKPA - p_band) x V x GAS_SCALE / (crew x R_B))` (sealed bay, valve closed, door closed) ; `t_pool_steps = ceil(pool_mGU / (LEAK_MGU + VENT_HOLD - R_SCR_served))` (valve open, pool draining) ; the isolated-erosion clock is D.14

**Variables:**
| Variable | Type | Range | Source | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `P0_DKPA`, `p_band` | int dkPa O2 | 210; THIN 180, HYPOXIC 140, ANOXIC 100 | LSG D.15 O2 bands | Normal and band boundaries |
| `V` | int tiles | 42 (`cryo_bay`) | C.1 | Room volume |
| `GAS_SCALE` | int | 1000 | LSG D.2 | mGU per dkPa per tile |
| `crew`, `R_B` | int | 1 to 2; 300 mGU per step per crew | LSG D.15 fixture | Occupants and respiration |
| `pool_mGU` | int | 3,000,000 (`ot_main`) | DA-35 | Authored O2_TANK stock |
| `LEAK_MGU`, `VENT_HOLD` | int mGU per step | 250, 300 | LSG D.15 O2-SHIP fixture `[verified by the systems-designer; Graph owner to confirm]` | Leak at `hp` 2000 and the vents holding 210 dkPa |
| `R_SCR_served` | int mGU per step | 0 (unserved), 200 or 400 | IDB:189 vs IDB:1105; `[UNV: Graph, ASK-G3]` | Scrubber contribution when served |

**Output Range:** the sealed clock exists only while someone breathes in the sealed bay (leaving through the door stops it); the pool clock runs from the moment the valve is opened.
**Example:** sealed, one crew: THIN ceil(30 x 42 x 1000 / 300) = 4,200 steps = **7.0 minutes**, HYPOXIC 9,800 steps = 16.3 minutes, ANOXIC 15,400 steps = 25.7 minutes; two crew halve each (co-op hook, Edge Cases). Pool drain with the scrubber unserved: ceil(3,000,000 / 550) = 5,455 steps = **9.1 minutes** (LSG's 5,455 +-15 %); with the scrubber served the net is 350 per step (8,572 steps = 14.3 minutes) at `R_SCR` 200 or 150 per step (20,000 steps = 33.3 minutes) at 400 (ASK-G3). Isolated `d_cryo_exposed`: 125 periods = 20.8 minutes of HOME time, no tell (D.14).

### D.20 Derelict B's hull-fatigue clock

The `hull_fatigue_clock` formula is defined as:

`periods_to_breach = ceil((hp0 - (BREACH_HP - 1)) / d_fat)` ; `A_j = ceil(A_HOLE_MAX x (BREACH_HP - hp_j) / BREACH_HP)` with `hp_j = BREACH_HP - 1 - d_fat x j` ; `m = smallest j such that 0.01 x sum of A_i for i = 1..j >= ln(P0 / P_band)` ; `periods_to_decomp = periods_to_breach + m`

**Variables:**
| Variable | Type | Range | Source | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `hp0` | int bp | 6999 (`seg_b_pump_n`, `fatigued`) | DA-11, DA-15 | Authored segment `hp` |
| `d_fat` | int bp per period | 100 (DERELICT) | D.15 | Fatigue wear |
| `A_HOLE_MAX` | int | 64 (range 32 to 128) | LSG D.2 | Hole area at `hp` 0 |
| `ln(P0 / P_band)` | fixed ratio | 0.916 | LSG D.15 HULL-FATIGUE decompression criterion (quoted, not re-derived) | The pressure fall that counts as DECOMP |

**Output Range:** breach after 30 periods (5.0 minutes of B-loaded time); DECOMP after 40 (6.7 minutes).
**Example:** `periods_to_breach` = ceil(3000 / 100) = 30 (`hp` 3,999 at period 30, `A_hole` 1); the hole then grows 2, 4, 5, 7, 9, 10, 12, 13, 15, 17 (exact D.2 area) and the sum passes 91.6 at `m` = 10 (sum 94; the Graph's D.15 shorthand `ceil(1.6 j)` gives the same `m`, sum 92). DECOMP at period 40 = 4,000 steps = **6.7 minutes**, against LSG D.15's DERELICT row of 6.2 minutes (3,750 steps): +6.7 %, inside +-15 %. The clock runs from the first loaded step, is cumulative across visits (dormancy freezes it, LS-17), and never stops after the weld because B stays DERELICT-kind inside the home body; the A hull plate (a FULL repair) stops it for good.

### D.21 Derelict B's restart reservoir floor

The `restart_reservoir_floor` formula is defined as:

`leak_ml(hp) = min(stock, ceil(LEAK_ML_MAX x (LEAK_HP - hp) / LEAK_HP))` ; `S0_min(W) = S_FLOOR + leak_ml(hp_restart) x 10 x W` ; `RES_FLOOR iff res_b_stock < S0_min(RES_B_REACTION_WINDOW_S)` evaluated at the authored `hp` ; `n_race = floor((hp_build - hp_race_min) / dp)` where `hp_race_min = LEAK_HP - floor(LEAK_HP x floor((stock - S_FLOOR) / (10 x W)) / LEAK_ML_MAX)`

**Variables:**
| Variable | Type | Range | Source | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `LEAK_ML_MAX` | int mL per step | 20 | LSG D.2 | Leak at `hp` 0 |
| `hp_restart` | int bp | 2900 at build | DA-17 (`pb_leak`) | The dead leak's `hp` when the pump first flows (V12) |
| `S_FLOOR` (this GDD's name) | int mL | 7,500 `[UNV: Graph D.8 circ-vs-level curve]` | read from LSG D.15's CLT-LEAK row (12,500 of 20,000 mL lost leaves `circ` about 437 permille) | Stock below which the loop heats toward OVERHEAT |
| `W` | int s | 90 (45 to 180) | Authoring knob `RES_B_REACTION_WINDOW_S` | Seconds to find the frost fan and complete a patch (3 s channel) |
| `res_b_stock` | int mL | 16,000 (authored) | DA-35 | RESERVOIR `stock_ml` |

**Output Range:** 0 to 20,000 mL; the authored stock must cover the floor plus the window's leak.
**Example:** `pb_leak` at `hp` 2900: `leak_ml` = ceil(20 x 1100 / 4000) = ceil(5.5) = **6 mL per step**; `S0_min(90)` = 7,500 + 6 x 10 x 90 = **12,900 mL**, at most the authored 16,000 mL (margin 3,100). Sensitivity: at the window's end `hp` has fallen 9 periods x 32 = 288 to 2,612 and the leak is 7 mL per step (13,800 mL, still under 16,000). The same formula explains why 2200 was rejected: `leak_ml` 9 at build and no slack. Under the literal D.14 reading the dry leak keeps wearing while B is loaded, so the race "V12 first, patch later" closes: the largest `leak_ml` the stock can carry is floor(8,500 / 900) = 9 mL per step, `hp_race_min` = 4000 - floor(4000 x 9 / 20) = 2200, and `n_race` = floor((2900 - 2200) / 32) = 21 periods = 3.5 minutes of B-loaded time. After that the sequence is **patch `pb_leak` first, then V12** (`SCRAM_RESTART_STEADY` uses that order; skipping the patch re-SCRAMs `r_b` within 90 s +-15 s). Under the gated reading (ASK-G1) the race never closes.

## Edge Cases

Every case states the exact outcome and, where an owner decides, names the owner; "lint" always means the CONTENT lint of DA-40 unless a Layout id is cited. Cases that depend on an open ask say which branch binds until it is answered. Figures are the Formulas section's.

**Zero, maximum and out-of-range authored values**

- **If a room authors zero fixtures**: `FIXTURE_COUNT_ROOM` ERROR naming the room, its `V` and the bound (`fixtures_min` 1); a dark housing counts, so the fix is adding a housing, not a powered lamp. A room with no lamp housing has no way to be re-lit or un-lit, which breaks restraint (P3) (D.3, EM-13).
- **If a room authors more fixtures than `min(6, 1 + floor_div(V, 8))`** (for example a third lamp in a `V` 20 dock): `FIXTURE_COUNT_ROOM` ERROR with the count and bound; no auto-trim. The bound is Layout's D.16 `[PLACEHOLDER: Emitter AC-58]`; a retune by the Emitter reruns the lint against the new text through `TUNING_SOURCE_DRIFT`.
- **If `DRIED_AFTER_STEPS` is retuned inside its registered range 7500 to 36000**: at 14,400 or below every dead fault still passes; above it `DEAD_FAULT_NOT_DRIED` fails `pa_frozen`, `j_a_main`, `pb_leak` and `seg_b_breach`, naming Failure & Trail as owner (the intended tripwire, D.11). Back-dating to -(36000 + 2400) = -38400 covers the whole range, but 38,400 steps (1 h 4 min) of `since_steps` at step 0 is outside what F&T registers as a trail input, so F&T must confirm it clamps rather than extrapolates `[UNV: F&T]`; recommended only after the Graph answers the sign ask (OQ-DA-1).
- **If `fail_since_step` is negative and M1 `instantiate_section` rejects it**: dead faults cannot be back-dated at build. Fallbacks in order: a Graph `dead_since` template field; or authoring the fault live and letting `since_steps` accrue (the first 20 loaded minutes), which breaks "dried on arrival" and keeps FT AC-44 BLOCKED. The BOOT subset's `DEAD_FAULT_NOT_DRIED` stays valid on either field.
- **If any authored number is a JSON float** (for example `2161.0` or `0.15`): `AUTHORED_NON_INTEGER` ERROR in CI; at load the cast-and-bound-check refuses a non-integral value and never rounds silently, because the engine's JSON parser returns floats `[UNV: 4.7.2, spike]`. Percent never appears: charges are dJ (DA-4).
- **If an authored `hp` is outside 0 to `HP_FULL` 10000 or a `stock` is negative**: the loader's bound check refuses the template whole at BOOTING (Layout's whole-template rejection, LY-30); the offline lint reports the range before the build can ship.
- **If `cab_trunk` `cap` is authored outside 2159 to 2163 W**: `OVERLOAD_WINDOW` ERROR naming the failing beacon state, its `rho_pm` and `dhp_ovl` (2150 W fails with the beacon on: rho 1116, 5 bp per step; 2000 W gives rho 1190 and DESTROYED in 71 s). The window is solved by D.9, so the fix is the number, never a lint waiver.
- **If a segment is authored at `hp` 0 (`seg_b_breach`) and Failure & Trail answers that a DESTROYED subject emits no descriptor**: the open hole cannot be drawn from the Graph's hull row (a P1 lie by omission) and the dried-trail count thins by one. Fallback order (Checkpoint rulings Q9): `seg_b_breach` at `hp` 1500 (FAILED, BREACH mode, a descriptor exists, patchable with zero parts, B's traverse kept because the room stays authored at vacuum); then a dead non-leaky power fault on a branch V12 never serves. Never a fault that wakes on V12, which would be a ninth live clock and break `SCRAM_RESTART_STEADY` (OQ-DA-11).
- **If home LOADs reach 12** (one more lamp, motor or heater): `NODE_COUNT_DRIFT` WARN, because 11 is already over the target 8 by 3 and the tolerance is `max(floor(8 x 20 / 100), 3)` = 3 (D.2). One more LOAD also adds 20 W to the trunk (the standing load 1880 W changes and D.9 must be re-solved) and a hardpoint.

**Faults, clocks and staging**

- **If the player never opens `v_o2_cryo`** (the closed-valve staging, literal LSG D.9): `d_cryo_exposed` is DESTROYED (`NOT_REPAIRABLE`) at minute 20.8 of HOME time with no tell, and `G0_ZERO_PART_SURVIVABLE` fails the closed-valve staging (ASK-G1, DA-16); therefore the closed-valve staging ships only if the Graph owner accepts ASK-G1 before MVP content lock; if ASK-G1 is declined or still unanswered at content lock, the valve-OPEN fallback ships (`v_o2_cryo` OPEN at wake, DA-16).
- **If the player closes a valve upstream of any LEAK-mode subject** (V5 used as a lever): `intensity_pm` drops to 0 (the drip, frost growth and hiss stop), mode LEAK stays, and `hp` follows the ASK-G1 branch (DA-16): a silent clock when literal, held when gated. The authored levers (DA-15 "State at build and lever") are written for the branch that binds.
- **If the player stays in the sealed `cryo_bay`** (door CLOSED, `v_o2_cryo` CLOSED, one crew): the bay's O2 reaches THIN at 7.0 minutes, HYPOXIC at 16.3 and ANOXIC at 25.7; leaving through the door ends the clock because the corridor has served vents. This is a consequence of the closed-valve staging, not a ninth fault, and its tell is the Graph's atmosphere band descriptors; it is the reason the cryo door is a short walk from the bed (D.19).
- **If a heater is installed in `hp_cargo_heater` while `j_cryo` is still open**: trunk load is 1240 + 500 = 1740 W, rho 805, no damage. The overload exists only for the pair (heater installed, cryo branch closed): closing `j_cryo` adds the 640 W branch and lands on 2380 W, rho 1101, the authored PWR-OVL (D.9). The trigger is a player act twice over, so the fault is LATENT in the sense of DA-15 (`FAULT_LEVER_UNREACHABLE` requires both acts reachable in sortie order).
- **If Attention switches the beacon on or off during the PWR-OVL burn**: `bcn_main` toggles 20 W, `rho_pm` moves between 1101 and 1110, `dhp_ovl` stays 4, so the 4.2-minute DESTROYED clock does not change; `set_beacon` off issues M14 on `bcn_main` only and no lamp is zeroed (DA-24).
- **If the cryo branch is re-energised from a full blackout** (every LOAD re-energised at once): requested power is clamped to `S` 2500 W `[PLACEHOLDER: Item DB out_w_rated]`, `cab_trunk` sees rho 1156 for `INRUSH_STEPS` 5 at 9 bp per step: 45 bp of scorch per recovery, so 67 recoveries are needed to cross below `HP_OK`. Breaker cycling is a small cost by design (D.9).
- **If the player closes the seam breaker while `r_b` is SCRAMmed** (S3 or S4): B's bus (1240 W) hangs below `cab_trunk`, L 3120 W, supply-clamped to S 2500 W, rho 1156, 9 bp per step, `cab_trunk` DESTROYED (`NOT_REPAIRABLE`) in 1,112 steps (about 1.9 minutes). Not a ninth fault, because it is an exogenous player act with a visible tell (the dimming home lamps and the trunk scorch, D.9); the seam breaker is authored OPEN at weld and the design rule is to close it after V12 (DA-20). `OVERLOAD_WINDOW` prints the figure as a WARN.
- **If a second `mod_scrubber` is ever hung below `cab_trunk`** (no MVP hardpoint accepts one: scrubber hardpoints are one per section): +600 W gives rho 1147, 8 bp per step and DESTROYED in 1,250 steps (2.1 minutes), outside the authored fixture by design. Content that adds such a hardpoint must re-solve D.9; the lint flags the new window failure, not the player's act.
- **If a PATCH is applied to `seg_b_pump_n` at `hp` 6999** (above `PATCH_CAP_BP`): M7 clamps by `hp_cap`, the patch adds nothing and `fatigued` stays set `[UNV: Graph whether hp_cap is lowered]`. Only a FULL repair (the A hull plate) stops the fatigue (LSG:237); Authoring never relies on a pre-breach patch (D.15).
- **If the player welds B without carrying the A hull plate**: `seg_b_pump_n` runs a 3.5-minute treadmill for the rest of the session (about 17 patches per hour at 210 s per cycle); G-0 holds and `WELD_WITHOUT_PLATE_SURVIVABLE` passes at that cadence. The dependency is stated, never gated (anti-pillar: no scripted sortie order).
- **If the player skips the `pb_leak` patch, restarts `r_b` and tries V12 again right after it re-SCRAMs** (Interaction's dead-reactor beat, IA:54): the first V12 succeeds (`T_mach` is below `OVERHEAT_OFF_MC` at build, DA-35); the re-SCRAM follows within 90 s +-15 s with `T_mach` above `OVERHEAT_OFF_MC`, and an immediate second V12 fills the bar and returns `BLOCKED(STILL_HOT)` until the machine cools. This is the only STILL_HOT case on B and it is the player's doing, not an eighth or ninth fault.
- **If the player runs V12 on B after the race window** (more than 21 periods, 3.5 minutes of B-loaded time, with `pb_leak` unpatched, literal reading): the leak exceeds 9 mL per step against a 16,000 mL stock, `circ` collapses, `r_b` re-SCRAMs within 90 s +-15 s. Past 15.2 minutes `pb_leak` is DESTROYED and `NOT_REPAIRABLE`, so the restart chain is unreachable until a part is spent; under the gated reading none of this happens (D.21, ASK-G1).
- **If a dead leaky fault reaches DESTROYED while its section is loaded**: it yields its `scrap_count` of parts on salvage (IDB-29), its edge `cap` is 0 and the dried trail's source is gone; `TRAIL_NO_SOURCE` treats a DESTROYED source as valid (the cause remains). `PART_SUPPLY_EXCEEDS` bounds placements statically, and the headless run counts instances, which may exceed 5 (DA-25).
- **If `pa_frozen` thaws** (the room warms above `FREEZE_OFF_MC` -10000 after the bus wakes `htr_a`): it leaves forced FREEZE and, in mode LEAK at `hp` 2500, wears at 32 bp per period: DESTROYED in 79 periods = 13.2 minutes of A-loaded time, and wakes with the coolant. Feeding A is therefore a clock decision twice over (DA-13 (b)). `[UNV: Graph]` whether a forced-FREEZE pipe counts as mode LEAK.
- **If the stored `res_a` or `rad_a` is not forced FREEZE** in `a_machine` (`T0` -22000, below `FREEZE_ON_MC` -20000): it would freeze-fail at load, an unlisted endogenous transition. `FROZEN_ROOM_UNFORCED` ERROR; every liquid-bearing node in any room whose `T0` is below `FREEZE_ON_MC` carries `forced_mode` FREEZE (DA-19, DA-36).
- **If a derelict is loaded for more than `T_SORTIE_LOADED_MAX` minutes idle**: the literal clocks apply (`pb_leak` DESTROYED at 15.2 minutes, `seg_b_pump_n` breached at 5.0 and decompressed at 6.7); the headless tests assert the ledger, not a promise that a long stay is safe (D.14, D.20).

**Simultaneous rules and cross-system collisions**

- **If the player sorties to A after the weld** (S4 `{home+B, A}`): `S_int` is 144 against `INTERACT_FIXTURE_BUDGET` 120 `[PLACEHOLDER: Interaction]`, an overshoot of 24; `SUBJECT_BUDGET_SET` is WARN with the shortfall (ERROR on S0 to S3). The Emitter's own cap is not reached (`S_total` 174 of `MAX_EXPOSURE_SUBJECTS` 192 `[PLACEHOLDER: Emitter]`, 18 to spare while actors and horrors stay at 6), so nothing fails dark at runtime today. Unless ASK-I1 (register by proximity or body adjacency) lands, ship the S4 diet below (D.17, OQ-DA-20).
- **If Authoring must cut 24 subjects from S4 without changing another owner's number** (Checkpoint rulings Q4 option 3; it needs only the Graph's confirmation that a VENT or LOAD may be authored bare, which DA-19 already assumes for junctions, OQ-DA-23): the named diet is 25 subjects, in this order: (1) **11 vents become bare VENT nodes** with no `def_id` and no hardpoint, like the bare junctions (home 5, A 2, B 4); (2) **11 non-residual wreck lamps become bare housings** on their branch with no swappable module (A 5, B 6); (3) **3 door motors become bare LOAD nodes** (`dm_cryo_s`, `dm_air_hatch`, `dm_b_hatch`). S4 `S_int` falls from 144 to 119, one under the budget. Readability cost: those 25 can no longer be cut, swapped or salvaged, so the module faucet loses 25 low-value instances (no parts, no scrubber, no breaker) and a dead wreck lamp cannot be replaced; switching still works because `set_switch` acts on the emitter. Diet (1) also takes the home-ship from 27 hardpoints to 22, which sits under `MAX_HARDPOINTS_PER_SECTION` 32 with 10 spare; the cap no longer forces the diet (C-4 was closed by the raise to 32), so diet (1) is justified by the S4 subject budget alone (OQ-DA-23). `[UNV: Layout, Graph]` whether a VENT, LOAD or housing node may be authored bare; this is the fallback only.
- **If Interaction accepts ASK-I1**: `SUBJECT_BUDGET_SET` reads the registered set (subjects within body adjacency or proximity) instead of the loaded set, the diet is not applied, and a far welded section no longer spends the budget.
- **If a joint ask is taken instead** (budget 144 with `MAX_EXPOSURE_SUBJECTS` 224 `[PLACEHOLDER: Emitter]`): `S_total` 174 passes with 50 spare; the change is two owners', registered by both (D.17). Raising the Interaction budget alone breaks I-7.
- **If the player opens the door between `a_spine` (-12000 mC) and `a_machine` (-22000 mC)**: conduction (`K_OPEN`) equalises toward the midpoint over minutes, `a_machine` warms and the spine cools; "exactly one lethal-cold room" is a **build-state** invariant, so `COLD_ROOM_DRIFT` prints the open-door projection and `a_dock`'s 26.9-minute figure is re-read with that door open (D.18).
- **If a Suit cell is topped up from `b_a1` or `b_b1`**: `withdraw_store` removes the credited stock one for one; the residual lamp's remaining loaded time is `stock / 20` steps and at 0 it dims for one step and goes dark. The honest reading is the player's own spend (P1); Authoring never relies on the lamp as a long-lived beacon (D.6).
- **If the residual lamp's tie junction (`j_a_res`, `j_b_res`) is closed on a powered bus**: the island joins the bus and the lamp is served by the bus; once the bus has surplus the battery recharges at `min(S - sum_served, CH_CAP)` (17.6 minutes for B from 12 %). The authored tie is OPEN at build so recharge is a V3 decision, never a free lamp (DA-27).
- **If a fixture housing, prop or loose slot sits on a fault's maximum floor run**: `LOOSE_ON_TRAIL_RUN` WARN (a slot within 1 tile of a floor run) or VA-5 review failure (a prop or housing); the trail must be readable from across the room (VA-4).

**Degenerate and exploit strategies**

- **If the player powers nothing on A and only cuts `scr_a` by beam**: legal and intended (DA-28's quiet alternative): no lantern lights, no clock starts on the dead branch, the spare breaker stays on its shelf, and the player takes the cut by beam and decides whether the failed scrubber is worth carrying home (`scr_a` stays the garbage beat; Boarding & Salvage BS-17, C-BS-6). Restraint is a real strategy, not a loss state (P3).
- **If the player strips every module node on both wrecks**: nothing respawns (one placement is one take, IDB-29); a wreck stripped bare is a dark place with less in it, not a "cleared" state (no win state). In the MVP, with two wrecks and no procedural generation, this is the end of authored content, not a win; scarcity then pushes inward only through the Vertical Slice's derelicts (OQ-DA-24).
- **If the player farms glowsticks**: 12 are placed once (home 8 in two stacks of 4, A 2, B 2), none respawn, and a carried stack is at most `GLOWSTICK_STACK_MAX` 4 `[PLACEHOLDER: Item DB]`. The lights-off strategy is not starved because the beam is hands-free (user decision).
- **If the player seals every door and stands in the scrubber room** (the Graph's D1 bot): the room enters THIN as the Graph's criterion asserts; authoring gives no safe farm: scrubbers wear under duty and a derelict scrubber is at 2000 or 6000 `hp` (LSG D1).
- **If the player patches `j_a_main` instead of swapping the spare breaker**: the junction reaches `PATCH_CAP_BP` 6000 and the bus wakes at DEGRADED, WORN trace on; the spare then sits on the shelf for the Vertical Slice. Both routes are legal (G-0, IDB AC-42); the swap is cheaper to keep.
- **If the player carries a cut module (for example `scr_a`) through a room with a live endogenous fault**: speed is `V_CARRY` 1, escape slack at 480 px falls to 2.0 s, and a route over 600 px cannot be carried inside the window; `ESCAPE_ROUTE_CARRY` WARN lists the room (D.5). The stowed hull plate is not a carry: it shows the HEAVY back-frame and leaves speed unchanged.
- **If the player powers A's bus and the battery runs out**: `b_a2` lasts 90 s with the full bus and 144 s with the scrubber cut; then the lanterns go dark, the pump stalls again and `htr_a` stops heating the lethal room. "Feeding the wreck is how you survive staying" has a battery's lifetime, not a base's; A has no reactor (D.16).
- **If the player takes the residual-lamp island battery to power something else**: `b_a1` and `b_b1` are `mod_battery_cell` modules on hardpoints and can be cut like any module; the wreck's unknown goes dark and the exterior view reads it dark (P1: the light had a source and the source left).

**Save, restore, load-dormancy and template edits**

- **If a derelict is unloaded mid-clock** (the pod leaves): its slots are skipped, `hp`, stocks, `warn_since_step` and the lamp's battery freeze exactly, and on return the section resumes with no fast-forward (LS-17). Every loaded-time clock (D.6, D.14, D.20) accumulates across visits, which is why a long absence never costs the player a wreck.
- **If a template is edited after a save exists**: its `template_hash` changes (LY-26). Before the first external build, saves are disposable (SL-14) and the old save is discarded; after it, migration belongs to the owner. A `room_key` rename is committed only with a note in the file header (DA-44).
- **If a negative `fail_since_step` is persisted and restored**: it is a signed persisted node field (LS-3, LS-21) and returns exactly; `since_steps` is recomputed from `step_no`, so a save made after the dried threshold keeps its dried trail and a back-dated fault never "re-wets".
- **If a world is restored from an autosave taken after the weld**: the sockets read WELDED, `mvp_world.json` is consulted only at world build, and no Authoring lint runs at restore; the BOOT subset runs at every boot, and the content of a restored node is the saved value, never the template's initial value (DA-35 is the build diff only).
- **If the player dies and restarts from the last autosave**: every Authoring-owned fact comes back as saved (clocks, stocks, charges); Authoring adds no death rule and no one-way point (DA-29). MVP death handling is Save/Load's and is cited, not restated.
- **If a residual lamp's section has never been built**: the exterior view reads `charge_dJ` > 0 as lit; the first load starts the clock and the persisted stock then governs (D.6, `[provisional: Web Map & Transit undesigned]`).

**Geometry, weld and topology**

- **If the weld is attempted while a lane's node is missing or its port type is wrong** (no DUCT-port node at the O2 lane, no CABLE-port node at the power lane): the Graph's `can_join` returns `PORT_INCOMPAT` and nothing is joined; DA-10 requires both lane nodes to be real and in the dock rooms so the seam edge stays far under `TRAIL_EDGE_MAX_PX` 1152.
- **If `offset_b` is not a multiple of 24 or any B room overlaps a home room**: Layout's `can_attach` reports the lattice error or `GEOM_OVERLAP` (the Graph's `can_join` is not reached); with the authored sockets the offset is (696, 384) and no rect pair overlaps (D.13).
- **If the player opens `door_b_pump_breach` while `b_pump` is pressurised and `b_breach` is vacuum**: the draught across the door is the tell; this is an exogenous act of the player's, so the fairness gate does not apply (LS-13), and the retreat is a closable door within 384 px of `seg_b_pump_n`'s anchor. The room is authored at vacuum so no precursor is hidden (DA-28).
- **If `seg_b_pump_n` breaches while the player is in `b_pump` with `door_b_spine_pump` CLOSED**: only the `V` 20 room decompresses; ANOXIC arrives about 10 s before DECOMP; the player is the only occupant, the hairline-crack-rib ladder has run for at least `W_MIN_WARNING_S` 10 s, and the door is 384 px (128 ticks running) away (D.5, D.20).
- **If a spawn cell lies within 1 tile of a fault's maximum floor run or fails `can_place`**: `SPAWN_BLOCKED` ERROR; the pod arrival rows are one tile clear of the docked-podcraft prop (DA-3).
- **If a room has no path back to `entry_room_key`** (a template edit severs the tree): `ROOM_UNREACHABLE` (Layout) or `RETURN_PATH_TRAVERSE` (Authoring's, for the vacuum or cold stretch); there is no authored one-way point, because death is lethal including friendly fire and the restart is from an autosave (DA-29).
- **If a vacuum or cold stretch's round trip exceeds 56 tiles** (for example a longer `b_breach`): `TRAVERSE_TOO_LONG` ERROR even though Suit's own one-way walking bound (112) still passes (D.7).

**Hallucination interplay (P4)**

- **If the Perception Filter reports a dead branch as "fixed" or "running"**: the same compartment carries a consequence-channel witness (dark fixtures, a static Graphite pipe pattern, a lantern off, no frost change); a low-sanity player can check by looking at the lamp. `TRUTH_WITNESS_MISSING` is WARN in MVP and ERROR once the Filter exists (DA-34).
- **If the Filter fabricates a second lit point or erases the residual light in the exterior transit view**: that is a lie about a real emitter and is forbidden by contract (routed ask to the Filter and Web Map & Transit); the data side is guarded by exactly one `residual: true` fixture per wreck and zero per home (`RESIDUAL_LIGHT_COUNT`, BOOT).
- **If a low-sanity player sees the failed scrubber "pristine" or a phantom salvage object** (Vertical Slice class d): the field check is the closed-list witnesses (the verdict trail's absence or presence, the dark lamp) plus SCAN (Boarding & Salvage BS-17), with undisturbed dust as supporting verdict grammar. A phantom has no record in a template, so nothing authored can be mistaken for it (guard rail G5).
- **If a crewmate appears as a horror in a 3-tile corridor** (class c, Vertical Slice): the corridor lets two bodies pass without contact, every room is inside the fairness budget of a closable door and a return path exists; lethal friendly harm is system-gated by Sanity and Interaction, and nothing authored creates an unavoidable contact (DA-29).
- **If a hallucination redraws a door as "welded" or "cut"**: authored interior doors only ever carry the closed and open leaf states, so a welded-looking leaf is itself the lie, and the leaf state is a Layout presentation fact the player can test by trying the door (DA-8).

**Co-op later hooks**

- **If two crew breathe in the sealed cryo bay**: the sealed clocks halve (THIN at 3.5 minutes, HYPOXIC 8.2, ANOXIC 12.8); seats 2 to 4 are Vertical Slice and are not authored, and the lints assume one crew until `spawn_cryo_2..4` exist (D.19, DA-3).
- **If seats 2 to 4 are added in the Vertical Slice**: they spawn in the cryo bay's open-dark pods, `WAKE_SINGLE_LIGHT` and `SPAWN_BLOCKED` re-run for each, and `ACTORS` 4 in the subject budget already covers four bodies (D.17).
- **If a placed reward requires another player to stand in a hazard to take it**: that would be an incentive to PvP-adjacent play (anti-pillar); the review check is that every part and module can be taken by the player who holds it, with the lethal cold room's `part_cable_splice` reached by a 5.6-second crossing and out again (D.10).

**Lint, tooling and tests**

- **If a CI content lint fails and the BOOT subset passes**: the build is blocked from merging, but a release boot is never bricked beyond the six BOOT rules (DA-41).
- **If two lint runs over the same bytes print different output**: a determinism defect; CI compares the two byte for byte, and the output is ordered by `(template_id, lint_id, record_key)` with no timestamp or machine-dependent field.
- **If a waiver names `STORY_SLOT_FILLED_IN_MVP`, or any waiver has no reason**: ERROR; there is no waiver for story slots in an MVP template, and a waiver row must carry a reason and an owner.
- **If a LOCKED row of `content_lint_config` differs from the registry** (for example `DERELICT_FAULT_COMPRESSION` edited): `TUNING_SOURCE_DRIFT` ERROR naming the row, the owner and both values; no auto-fix. A PLACEHOLDER row that differs from its owner's text is the same ERROR, so a placeholder cannot silently become a fact.
- **If an unknown `def_id` is placed**: `PLACEMENT_DEF_UNKNOWN` fails the offline lint and, as a BOOT rule, refuses the template at boot with the offending record; the runtime never meets a null def (IDB AC-90).
- **If a second self-powered lamp or a second beacon appears**: `SELF_POWERED_LAMP_COUNT` and `BEACON_COUNT` refuse at boot (EM:665); the cryo `fx_cryo_lamp` is the only `node_ref` 0 fixture in the world.
- **If a built template has more than 8 live faults or fewer**: `FAULT_COUNT_EIGHT` ERROR by `fault_id`; dead faults and authored states do not count, woken dead faults count for glow only (D.12).
- **If `tests/` and the gdUnit4 addon do not exist** (today): the lints run as the offline tool only and the headless tests are specifications; CI wiring is the project's test-setup story (IDB OQ 28), stated as the target, never as a passing gate.
- **If the home design edge count (34) is read against LSG's "about 55 edges" and "about 8 CABLEs"**: the edge total is INFO (below target), but the 16 power edges are twice the "about 8 CABLEs": 17 power nodes need 16 edges when every island is a tree (DA-9, DA-20). Reported as INFO, not linted; the Graph's MVP-content criterion ("about 40 nodes and about 55 edges") should read as an estimate (OQ-DA-22).

## Dependencies

Status vocabulary: **designed** (a GDD file exists, read in full for the rules cited), **in-flight** (a draft file exists and is changing), **undesigned-provisional** (no file; the contract is stated here and marked `(provisional — X undesigned)`). Direction is from this GDD's point of view. Disk state is the Glob of `design/gdd/` on 2026-10-26 when this part was written; after the sibling GDDs land, every provisional row is a confirming-sweep target.

| System | Direction | Strength | Interface (names as fixed by the owner) | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Ship Section & Layout Data Model | Authoring depends on it | **Hard** | the template schema (LY-4); the structural lint (LY-30); `compatible_defs` hook; `build_section`; `loose_slots`; `subject_anchors`; `route_length_px`; `room_adjacency`; D.13, D.16, D.18, D.19 | designed (the primary upstream) |
| Item & Module Database | Authoring depends on it | **Hard** | stable `def_id` strings (24 defs); IDB-24 dead-module convention; `hp_start`; IDB-19 part map; F-11 `part_cover_pm`; IDB-29 faucet and G-0 | designed |
| Emitter State & Light-Exposure Query | Authoring depends on it | **Hard** | emitter kinds; E1 `instantiate_section_emitters`; caps 192 / 96 / 10; EM-11, EM-14, EM-16, EM-24; AC-58 | designed |
| Life-Support Graph | Authoring depends on it | Soft (content contracts; the Graph has no row for Authoring) | LS-17, LS-25, LS-26; LSG D.9, D.12, D.15; M1 `instantiate_section`; `forced_mode`; `fail_since_step`; `DERELICT_FAULT_COMPRESSION` 500 | designed |
| Failure & Trail System | Authoring depends on it | Soft | FT-3, FT-11, FT-18, FT-37, FT-38, FT AC-44; `DRIED_AFTER_STEPS` `[PLACEHOLDER]` | designed; routed ask ASK-F&T-H1 (the heat-presentation step at `HEAT_TELL_STEP_MC`, DA-28, OQ-DA-37) |
| 2D Lighting & Occlusion | Authoring depends on it | Soft | LO-23, LO-33 / PD-1; `SHADOW_SLOTS_MAX` 6 `[PLACEHOLDER: Lighting]`; `CONTAIN_COLOURS` `[PLACEHOLDER]` | designed; routed ask ASK-F&T-H1 (co-owner of the b_reactor heat-presentation step, DA-28, OQ-DA-37) |
| Interaction System | Authoring depends on it | Soft | `place_loose`; `sync_section`; IA-37 budgets `[PLACEHOLDER]`; `set_switch` | designed |
| Suit & Equipment | Authoring depends on it | Soft | SE-21; SE D.11; AC-SE-75; `RESERVE_S`, `INSUL_MC` `[PLACEHOLDER]` | designed |
| Save/Load & Versioned Format; Command & Simulation Layer | Authoring depends on it | Soft | SL-14, SL-18; K-2 `init_world`; kernel H10 (headless) | designed; K-2 provisional |
| Pixel-Perfect Render Pipeline & Camera; art bible 6.0 to 6.6 and 8.2, 8.5 | Authoring depends on it | Soft | kit cells; face height at most 72; the up-screen convention; naming | designed |
| Player Controller & Input | Authoring depends on it | Soft | chest-point beam origin; lit-only channelled verbs (clearances measured from the chest point) | designed |
| Ship Body & Interior Physics | Authoring supplies it | Soft | spawn table; `can_place`; the `pod` template (BDY-20, BDY-25) | designed |
| Boarding & Salvage | depends on Authoring | **Hard** (it needs slots) | salvage slots, `def_id` placements with `hp`, `entry_room_key`, the loose ledger | on disk; node anchors and console open (OQ-DA-39, OQ-DA-40) |
| Web Map & Transit | depends on Authoring | **Hard** | `template_id` roster, `entry_room_key`, `visible_unknown`, `mvp_world.json`, spawn table; `residual_lamp_predicate`; `pod_body` | on disk; pod console open (OQ-DA-39) |
| Procedural Derelict Generation (VS-A) | depends on Authoring | **Hard** (Vertical Slice) | kit manifest, record shapes, lint table | undesigned-provisional |
| Environmental Storytelling Placement (VS) | depends on Authoring | Soft | the reserved story-slot list | undesigned-provisional |
| Modular Ship Assembly (on disk, In Design) | neighbour | Soft | socket records; plate positions; the staged install slot `hp_cargo_heater`; `socket_plan`, LM2 and LM3 are Layout's; the atomic apply and its call order are Assembly's; `can_join` the Graph's | on disk, In Design; asks open (OQ-DA-31 to OQ-DA-33) |
| Attention System | neighbour | Soft | the beacon data row (DA-24); the EMERGENCY cryo lamp (DA-23); `room_adjacency`, `route_length_px`, emitter placements; AT-6, AT-18, F-16 | designed |
| Horror Entities | neighbour | Soft | `room_adjacency`, `route_length_px`, emitter placements; `HORROR_SUBJECTS_MAX` 2; `DEAD_END_NARROW` (E-HE-26) | designed |
| Sanity; Perception Filter | neighbours | Soft | truth-only data; the falsifier map; `LIE_SURFACE_FLOOR`, `INSTRUMENT_NO_BINDING` | on disk |
| In-World Instruments & Inspect View | neighbour | Soft | the instrument placement record (IW-31); `INSTRUMENT_NO_BINDING`, `PATCH_OVER_OVERRIDE`, `LIE_SURFACE_FLOOR`; DA-34 witnesses | on disk; instrument rows open (OQ-DA-38) |
| Onboarding (Cryo Bay) | neighbour (VS) | Soft | the cryo bay as the fixture | undesigned-provisional |

**What each downstream or neighbour must repeat in its own GDD** (so the dependency is bidirectional): Boarding & Salvage repeats that the faucet is the authored node list and loose placements, the ledger is finite, and a failed module's field read is the closed-list witnesses plus SCAN (BS-17). Web Map & Transit repeats the reachable loaded sets (DA-32, including the post-weld sortie to A), that the pod is a Layout body built from the `pod` template, that a derelict left behind goes dormant when the pod leaves (never discarded), and the exterior-view rule for the residual light (`residual_lamp_predicate`). Procedural Derelict Generation repeats that it consumes the manifest and lint rows and does not fork them. Modular Ship Assembly (on disk) repeats the lane set and the weld offset (MSA-11 does). Attention (designed) repeats AT-18's one-beacon rule (it submits `set_beacon` to the one beacon the data holds and never moves or adds one) and that residual and EMERGENCY lamps are not modulated. The Perception Filter repeats that authored data is truth and that it must not fabricate or erase the residual light in the exterior view. Ship Body repeats DA-3 (the spawn table and the pod template).

Two dependencies are **circular-adjacent but not cyclic**: Layout names Authoring as the owner of the content lint while Authoring depends on Layout's schema (a one-way data edge each way: the schema flows down, the authored files flow up, and neither needs the other at runtime to start). The systems index lists three soft cycles; none touches this system.


## Tuning Knobs

Knobs are Authoring's own authored numbers and lint thresholds; every row names the formula or rule it comes from, so a balance pass changes the source and not the symptom. Defaults are the values written in the Detailed Design. **Solved** means the value is derived by the named formula and the range is the formula's legal interval, not a taste range. A `★` marks a value another GDD must cite by name; per R21 Authoring registers only its own artefacts (DA-44), so a `★` row is registered as the table or instance named there and never as a free constant. Owner-named values are in the pointer table after the main table and are not restated here. Rows marked **ask** are requests to another owner, not Authoring defaults.

## Visual/Audio Requirements

*Consulted: art-director (Stage 3 proposal, all of it integrated below), with the advisor rulings R13, R23 and Checkpoint rulings Q2, Q3 binding where the art-director and the level-designer differed. "ART n.n" is a section of `design/art/art-bible.md`. Numbers marked **PROPOSED** are art-director values for the art spike to tune; everything else is quoted from a locked source. Pixel-level frame budgets belong to the Pipeline and Lighting GDDs and are cited, not redefined.*

**Scope.** Derelict Authoring draws nothing and plays nothing. It authors data (floor plans, placement records, a kit manifest, a lint), so every visual here is a consequence of that data, drawn by the PROJECTOR (Layout), the Pipeline, Lighting, Failure & Trail or Emitter State. This section specifies (a) what the authored data must make true on screen, (b) the kit art the data names, and (c) the visual rules the content lint enforces (DA-39, DA-40). **Audio** is a citation, not a decision (last sub-section).

### Event inventory: what the authored data makes visible

Class **T** = truth channel (a Graph consequence or a structural fact; exact under the Perception Filter, which may add content but never move the real boundary, ART 1 principle 1). Class **C** = corruptible module-body channel (the Filter may substitute class (b) false states; every C item has a falsifier in the map below, P4). Classification of structure and props is `(provisional — Perception Filter undesigned)`; re-check when it lands (Layout OQ 13).

| # | Event | Source data | Draws | Class | Requirement (numbers, constraints) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| E1 | A section is instantiated | template, `mvp_world.json` | PROJECTOR (Layout) | T | The plan reads as ONE rect set: the drawn wall, door and prop extent equals the rect that stops the body and the beam (LY-7, LY-9). Floor at z 0, not Y-sorted; wall faces and props Y-sort by footprint south edge; caps never sort. Wall footprint `WALL_FOOTPRINT_DEPTH` 24; face at most 72 px for anything flush to a wall. |
| E2 | Cryo wake | EMERGENCY fixture, CLT-LEAK, dark heater | Lighting, F&T, module presenter | T | ART 2.2: ambient about 3 % on Graphite; `fx_cryo_lamp` Tungsten `#FFCC66` (`fixture_subkind_emergency`), dim, hanging low, shadow slot 6; the gutter is irregular dips of 0.3 to 0.5 s (18 to 30 ticks) no more often than every 10 to 20 s (600 to 1200 ticks), never periodic, never the deployable flicker. The frost run goes from the leak anchor to the far wall of the 7-tile room, inside `frost_reach(-2000 mC)` = 544 px at the F&T defaults, reachable at wake only because `p_cryo_rad` is back-dated to `fail_since_step` -60 (DA-15 #1; BLOCKED-ON OQ-DA-1) (F&T's smaller figure for a long-aged trail is a knob FLOOR, FT-11 / AC-23, never written as a reach here). The heater, the other cryo-bay fixture (`fx_cryo_2`, unserved behind the open breaker `j_cryo`) and the beacon are dark; adjoining rooms may be lit behind their closed doors (Checkpoint Q2). The pod row is strictly ordered; disorder only in which pods stand open. Nothing moves faster than the drip. |
| E3 | Power restored to a dead branch (V3 / V12) | Graph `served_pm`, fixture `switch_on` | Lighting recipe, Pipeline, Emitter | T | The lamp lights through Lighting's own warm-up recipe (a Lighting-owned cause); then the loop's hue appears along its pipe and the flow pattern (chevrons, dash-pulse, sight-glass) starts to move. A pipe on a dead branch is Graphite with a STATIC pattern (ART 4.2). No mask fade-in. Steps arrive at the Graph cadence (`GRAPH_STEP_TICKS` 6, 10 Hz), so the player can watch a wake travel down the pipe. The room floods with no new command (EM-13). |
| E4 | A dried (dead) fault is seen | DEAD fault record, `fail_since_step` | F&T (dull ramp D.5) | T | Final dull stops: coolant fan greyed with no drip; power scorch matte radial with a Hull Grey rim and no glow; hull hairline unchanged (integrity does not dull); O2 draws NOTHING (FT-18). No dried fuel stain on either wreck (not needed: the verdict grammar is served by coolant, power and hull). Every dried stop is at least 1.90:1 against Graphite lit `#5C6670` (`TRAIL_MIN_CONTRAST_X100` 190); interiors are edges and stains, never planes. Authored `-fail_since_step` at least `DRIED_AFTER_STEPS` `[PLACEHOLDER: Failure & Trail]` gives `dull_step` 3 of `DULL_STEPS` 4 and `glow_step` 0 at step 0. |
| E5 | A live fault starts | fault record | F&T | T | The s, m, l trail with its own 10 to 30 s tell. The plan supplies only the anchor and a clear floor run (VA-4). No decal, icon or arrow. At wake only CLT-LEAK shows from the wake room (DA-16). |
| E6 | A module sits at its authored `hp` | module placement `{def_id, hp}` | module presenter (IDB `sprite_family`) | **C** | Worn, scuffed, dark when FAILED; the same def at a lower `hp` (IDB-24). FAILED = rotor still, no medium moving, no lantern. A bolted patch plate only after a player PATCH. Module wear is `hp`-driven by the Item DB and is NOT the template `_worn` kit wear (below): a worn wall around a pristine module and a dark FAILED module in a maintained room are both legal. |
| E7 | A module is cut free | hardpoint record | Pipeline, Boarding & Salvage | T | The pedestal rect stays (LY-20) and severed ports show `open_end` stubs (IDB-7). Authored templates never dead-end a pipe (`PIPE_DEAD_END`); a cut stub is a runtime consequence and legal. Stub art is Boarding & Salvage's and the Item DB's, not counted here. |
| E8 | A loose item lies in a slot | `loose_slots` `{def_id, condition}` | Interaction `place_loose`, Pipeline | **C** (sprite) | In the item's own cell, Graphite plus its loop hue, no rarity glint, no pickup flash. Never on a trail's floor run (VA-5). Lit only by the beam or room lamps; not emissive. A real item has NO violet and NO flesh pixel (phantom salvage betrays itself by that leak, ART 4.2). |
| E9 | The residual light (interior, then from the pod) | `residual: true` FIXTURE, `visible_unknown` | Emitter, Lighting; Web Map & Transit (exterior) | T | One Tungsten FIXTURE `#FFCC66`, steady, dim, NOT guttering and NOT modulated by Attention (the gutter is the cryo lamp's grammar and Attention's tell; the exterior unknown must be a fixed, honest point). Brightness follows the Graph's served ratio on the lowest `power_tier` the def allows, never a hand-set glow (`-> OQ-V4`). Exterior: a single warm point in black through a port in the host room's hull-run wall. At battery exhaustion it simply goes out (a Graph consequence, never "fades for effect", LO named rule 2). Charges: 540,000 dJ on A, 432,000 dJ on B (45 and 36 minutes of loaded time, D.6). (orchestrator ruling: the level-designer's loaded-time charges supersede R7's 25 % / 75 min worked example, because LS-17 freezes an unloaded wreck; drift accepted at Checkpoint.) |

The kit still ships `cut` and `welded` for the seam and Vertical Slice. Cut shows ragged edges with hue-less Void Steel tool scorch on the side the cutter came from (exempt from the loop-scorch ban, VA-8), never Power Amber rimmed. The leaf slides whole pixels per tick into its wall pocket (LY-9). Frame = the Foundry round port, 96x96. |

DARK at wake. The lens is Graphite when off; the red lives only in the emitted light (no red albedo anywhere). Red appears nowhere else (ART 4.1). Exactly one on `home_ship`, `gates_actions` false (DA-24). |

Beyond the hole: black, no star, no nebula (ART 2.5). Web intrusion appears only here, from the hull side, under direct light (VA-11). Authored with small `since_steps` so the overlay and the emitter agree (`-> OQ-V3`). |
