### Palette: what each authored thing may be

| Authored thing | Albedo | Emission or light | Forbidden |
|---|---|---|---|
| Floor tiles | Graphite mid `#2A303A` plus one step (two values, pattern contrast one step, ART 6.6) | none | any chromatic hue; Hull Grey as a plane |
| Wall faces, caps, frames, brackets | Graphite (mid, lit `#5C6670`); Void Steel `#0B1018` in crevices (one value step) | none | Hull Grey except thin elements (edge scuffs, bolt rhythm, bead) |
| Lamp housing (FIXTURE) | Graphite; lens Void Steel with ONE tight highlight | Tungsten `#FFCC66` when served | red; any loop hue; any glow baked into the sprite (the lit state is the light; the sprite is identical when off) |
| EMERGENCY and residual lamps | as FIXTURE | `#FFCC66`, dim | an ice-blue cast (would read as coolant); iridescence; any loop hue at MVP (orchestrator ruling: Tungsten on both wrecks, V-7; a loop-hue residual is Vertical Slice) |
| Beacon | Graphite, lens Graphite when off | Alarm Red sweep only | red albedo |
| Pipes, dead branch | the loop's shape in Graphite, static pattern | none | loop-hue emission |
| Pipes, live branch | as above | O2 Cyan `#3FD6E8`, Power Amber `#F2C230`, Coolant Ice `#C8E6F5`, Fuel Ember `#F06A28`; integrity Hull Grey `#8C949C` on seams and ribs only | a hue on a pipe of another loop |
| Trails | F&T per loop; dried = D.5 final stops | F&T glow rows only | a trail not mapped to a Graph fault; a dried fuel stop at MVP |
| Pod props | Graphite; frost on the glass only; occupant silhouette Void Steel / Graphite behind the frost | none | skin tone; any flesh-band pixel; a face (ART 6.5) |
| Items, parts, modules | Graphite plus the loop hue the item mends; tools are silhouettes | none (a glowstick is inert until activated) | rarity colour; violet; flesh |
| Glowstick light | n/a | Chemlight `#86B896`, steady then waning, radius 2 tiles | any other use of this hue |
| Patch plates, seam bead, cap plate | Graphite body; Hull Grey only on bolt rhythm and a 4 px bead | none | a Hull Grey plane; hiding the bead |
| Web intrusion | no albedo; a shader band teal to green to bronze to gold, low saturation, only under direct light | none | the 270 to 330 degree arc; any unlit drawing |

**Sanity palette lint (asset level).** Reserved: violet (hue 270 to 330, S above 25 %) and flesh (hue 5 to 30, S 20 to 60 %, V 45 to 85 %). Authored content contains neither. Two checks, run by `tools/asset-pipeline/` (`PALETTE_SANITY_BAND`, run point ASSET): a static scan of every kit sheet and item sprite, and a lit sample of Hull Grey and lit Graphite under every Tungsten source whose hue must stay at 36 degrees or more (Hull Grey under `#FFCC66` computes to about hue 43, S 56 %, V 55 %, the closest sane colour to flesh, which is why it is a thin-element colour). Worked example: a 2 px `_worn` corner scuff under the EMERGENCY lamp samples at hue 36 or more; widened to a 6 px strip (a plane in practice) it fails review.

**The monochrome-wreck rule (measurable "darkness is honest").** Before any power is restored, the chromatic content of a derelict frame is a closed list: (1) the player's Tungsten beam, (2) the one residual lamp, (3) thrown Chemlight pools, (4) FAULT_GLOW of live faults (a dried stop carries no hue at MVP). Everything else is Graphite, Void Steel or Hull Grey. A wreck with ambient colour is a wreck that was lit for free (ART 1 principle 1). Tested by AC-DA-52.

**Luminance ladder.** Highest first: beam core Tungsten about 92 = Coolant Ice about 91 > Power Amber about 80 = O2 Cyan about 79 > Hull Grey about 61 = Fuel Ember about 61 > Alarm Red about 47 = Graphite lit about 43 > Graphite mid about 20 > Void Steel about 5 (L*; Chemlight about 70). Two consequences: no floor prop's lit albedo exceeds Graphite lit where a trail could run (`FLOOR_PROP_TOO_BRIGHT`, a Hull Grey prop beside a dried stop leaves no contrast to spare); and the residual lamp (`#FFCC66`, about 5 degrees from Power Amber) is told apart from a power bar by saturation, shape (a static housing on a wall, not a bar with a travelling dash) and behaviour (a fixture never carries a dash-pulse). A wreck room passes the achromatic pass when every hero (beam, media, seam, unknown, trail) separates by value or shape with colour removed (ART 4.7).

### Donor wear: the `_worn` commitment (answers Layout OQ-25)

**Two wear axes never mix.** *Kit wear* is driven by the TEMPLATE (which atlas it names), applies to floors, wall faces, caps, door frames and leaves, pipe mounts, fixture housings and props, and never changes at runtime. *Module condition* is driven by authored `hp` and later player patches, applies to `mod_` bodies, and changes at runtime.

**Closed `_worn` vocabulary** (inside ART 6.2): scuff-to-bare at rounded corners, dust (matte Graphite mid, the floor's second value, never a third), crevice darkening (Void Steel, one value step, direction-agnostic), clogged grates. **Excluded:** scorch, soot, char, fuel or coolant stains of any kind (those are Graph trails, FT-3 and FT AC-44; a static soot decal is a false history, P1), rust or oxidation (ART 6.2), warm stains, anything that reads as skin. (orchestrator ruling: Checkpoint Q3 adopts the narrowing; R23's "scorch/soot" is withdrawn; the ART 6.2 note is routed to the art-director, DA-5.)

| Cue | Maintained (`home_ship`) | `_worn` (A, B) **PROPOSED** | Survives the 50 % zoom-out step |
|---|---|---|---|
| Corner scuff | none; Graphite lit edge | Hull Grey bare edge, 2 px wide at every rounded corner of wall, door and prop pieces | yes (2x2 minimum feature, ART 5.6) |
| Floor dust | 0 to 5 % of tile area | 30 to 50 %, clustered at walls, corners and the lee of props, Graphite-mid blobs of at least 4x4 px | yes |
| Panel seam | crisp 1 px Void Steel line | the line plus 2 px of dust fill | marginal; carried by the other two |
| Grates | open | clogged with the dust value | yes |
| Fastener rhythm | intact big Foundry rivets at wide pitch | unchanged (a Foundry bolt is never missing; missing bolts are Lattice's axis) | n/a |

The `_worn` set keeps Foundry geometry, rivet rhythm, corner radius and panel ratio, so the three-piece test (ART 6.3) still identifies the dialect; only surface history differs. **Mechanism (behaviour only):** an atlas swap by template; `env_foundry_tiles_alb.png` and `env_foundry_tiles_worn_alb.png` share slice names and one `layout.json`, so the PROJECTOR picks the atlas by `template_id` and no Layout field carries wear (LY:1099); a decal overlay was rejected because it needs a placement record Layout lacks (guard rail G1). `WEAR_TOKEN_MISMATCH` fails a wreck that names a maintained piece or `home_ship` naming a `_worn` one. -> ADR candidates: worn-atlas derivation tool; atlas selection by `template_id`.

**The X-1 gate (Layout X-1).** Art-director commitment: MVP, Foundry only. In ONE lit capture at the seam with the doubled bulkhead in view and the same beam on both sides (so ambient differences cannot answer), at least 4 of 5 blind reviewers name the maintained side (AC-DA-07). If the cues need a third reviewer prompt, widen the dust coverage band before adding a new cue type. If the art spike cannot meet it, X-1 is downgraded to Vertical Slice in Layout's text, not left unsupported.

### Composition by area (ART 6.4 applied)

Counting conventions: DA-33.

| Area | ART 6.4 numbers | Worked example | MVP application |
|---|---|---|---|
| Cryo bay (`home_ship`) | wall props 1 to 2 per 10; floor at most 10 %; media 1; trails 1; strictly ordered pod row | 7 x 6 = 42 tiles gives at most 4 tiles of floor dressing; one coolant leak source = 1 trail; the drip is the 1 medium; the heater is a steady coil, not a medium; the exposed DUCT is a static pipe until it goes live | the pods ARE the wall (they count as wall) |
| Corridors | wall props at most 1 per 10; floor at most 5 %; media 0 | a 3 x 13 corridor = 39 tiles gives at most 1 tile (5 % of 39 is 1.95); head clearance 144 px at any anchor that can reach sev 4 (`CORRIDOR_HEAD_NARROW`, Layout's id) | a non-shadow FIXTURE sits at least 98 px from each interior face backing onto another compartment (C3, LO-33); a 3-wide corridor with interior faces on both long sides cannot meet it, so its fixture is placed anyway and logged PD-1, and a corridor with one hull side places its fixtures there (DA-31) |
| Machine rooms | wall props 3 to 4 per 10; floor 20 to 30 %; media 3 to 6; trails originate here; clear cross-path | a 5 x 10 room = 50 tiles gives 10 to 15 tiles of dressing; depth at most 5 | machinery on walls, media facing the room, face at most 72 px |
| Airlock, pod-dock | not in the table: corridor-class plus ONE 96x96 prop (docked podcraft placeholder) and the dock fitting | floor at most 5 % | the socket hatch is the hero; after a weld the bead is hero #3 |
| Dead derelict rooms | original type, displaced; 2 to 4 dried trails per breached section; at most 30 % torn pieces; 1 to 2 web entries; media 0 | B's breach section: of N wall and floor pieces at most 30 % take a torn or buckled state with no through-hole | "displaced" is rotation-free placement variety on the 24 px lattice, not an off-grid record (`-> OQ-V8`) |
| Web exterior | 0 props | n/a | Web Map & Transit's; Authoring supplies `visible_unknown` only |

**Home-ship lamp rhythm (ART 2.3, 2.4).** The home-ship reads domestic: many small overhead Tungsten fixtures, soft, shadowless (the six shadow slots are the four beams, the beacon and one more). Every room carries at least one housing so it can be re-lit AND un-lit (P3 restraint). Wall-mounted housings sit ABOVE the pipe band, on face rows 59 to 72 (14 px; **PROPOSED**, inferred from the locked band 29 to 58); the free-hanging housing is the EMERGENCY lamp only, drawn on the cap layer at 24x24 px or less and at least 1 tile off the coolant floor run so it never hides the hero-#5 trail (the layer question is `-> OQ-V9`). The conserved-dark and lost-dark moods (ART 2.4) are produced by Lighting from the same authored fixtures, not by a second template.

**One sweep answers three questions (ART 6.0).** For every room one beam sweep answers: who built this (Foundry: round ports, big rivets, squat panels); what did people do to it (patches, a missing module at a pedestal); has the web come in (only B's breach). Tested by AC-DA-93 (a).

**Verdict grammar at MVP (ART 6.5, DA-30).** With story slots empty, the three sentences are carried only by Graph facts and kit states: *Cause* = a dried trail leading to the failed fitting near the door the crew used (eye line trail, source, hatch); *Verb* = a door leaf state, deferred to Vertical Slice at MVP (interior doors are closed or open only); *Absence* = undisturbed dust, an empty pod (open-dark), a vacated pedestal where a module was cut cleanly. Suits and bodies are not MVP (ART 6.5: never skin, never a body). The three sentences are distributed across rooms, never inside one cone. Recommended minimum per wreck: one dried trail leading to a hatch, one pod or pedestal absence. This is a visual recommendation; pod-state assignment is the narrative-director's (OQ-DA-8; default one open-dark, the rest closed-frosted, no silhouette, DA-30).

### Animation and motion constraints

| Thing | Allowed | Not allowed | Why |
|---|---|---|---|
| Kit pieces | none | idle animation, bobbing, pulsing | ART 6.4 "nothing decorative moves" |
| Door leaf | slide whole pixels per tick into the pocket on the hinge side | rotation, easing, sub-pixel slide | integer pixel pipeline (LY-9) |
| Machinery moving medium (live) | the loop's own pattern in a region of at least 16x16 | motion on a dead module (rotor still) | ART 8.2, 4.2 "dead = static" |
| Pipe flow pattern | chevrons, dash-pulse, sight-glass, on live branches only; static on dead | animating on a dead branch | P1 |
| Frost, dried scorch, dried stain, crust | none (F&T may step the dull ramp) | shimmer, sparkle, pulse | ART 3.3 |
| Drip (cryo bay) | fall frames at the drip cadence | faster than "a drip" in the wake | ART 2.2 |
| EMERGENCY lamp | irregular gutter, 18 to 30 tick dips, no more often than every 600 to 1200 ticks | periodic flicker; the deployable flicker | ART 2.2 |
| Residual lamp | none (steady) | the cryo gutter, an Attention gutter, a power-down fade for effect | LO named rule 2: every change has a cause |
| Beacon | red sweep, 128 ticks per revolution, housing rotates only while emitting | strobe; rotation when dark | ART 2.4 |
| Seam bead | none; fresh to old at 36,000 ticks | pulse | ART 6.1 |
| Dust motes | slow, non-directional, 1 px, inside lit volumes of breathable compartments only (VA-15) | directional streaming; any motion in vacuum | F&T row 24 AIRFLOW is directional truth and must not be imitated |
| Zoom ladder | every piece integer-scaled; detail survives the 50 % step (2x2 minimum) | sub-pixel detail | Pipeline ladder, ART 5.6 |

### Truth-channel classification and the falsifier map (P4)

| Visual | Class | Reason |
|---|---|---|
| Walls, floors, caps, door frames, seam bead, cap plate, plates; door leaf state | T | geometry (one rect set); Graph `door_state` |
| Fixture, residual, EMERGENCY, beacon and glowstick LIGHT | T | real emitters with `lit` from the Graph (EM-11, EM-13); a false lamp is an unlit decoy overlay, never a light (LO). The housing sprite is C-capable, its light is T |
| Live and dried trails, AIRFLOW, frost crust, hole stamp, FAULT_GLOW rim | T | Graph descriptors only (FT-3); a dried trail is its own falsifier |
| Pipe flow pattern (moving or static) | T | live versus dead from the Graph |
| Pod props, pedestals, web intrusion | T `(provisional)` | authored scenery; the Filter may add phantom copies, never edit these |
| Module body at its `hp`; dead gauges on a module; loose item sprite | **C** | module-body channel; each needs the falsifier below |
| Dust motes | T-neutral | atmosphere; carries no information |

| Corruptible visual (authored) | What a hallucination could say | Truth-channel companion within one beam sweep |
|---|---|---|
| A's FAILED scrubber `scr_a` (`hp` 2000), dark rotor | "running, intact" | **No consequence pixel exists** (O2 failure leaves no trail, FT-18). Witnesses: the room is lamp-free (T; lamp brightness is on LSG:44's closed list); the dead O2 pipe on its branch is Graphite-static and the dust is undisturbed (S, supporting verdict grammar, VA-14; neither is on LSG:44's closed list). The falsifier is the closed-list witnesses plus SCAN (Boarding & Salvage BS-17, which answers OQ-V10). This is the single flagged exception; `TRUTH_WITNESS_MISSING` is WARN at MVP (DA-34). |
| A's broken breaker `j_a_main` (`hp` 2000) | "closed, live" | dried radial scorch at the box (T); no emission along the dead bus (T) |
| A's pump stall (PUMP, STARVE, dead branch) | "pumping" | STARVE draws nothing (F&T row 6): the pump's lantern is OFF (Emitter, T) and the wide-chevron pattern is static (T) |
| B's SCRAM reactor `r_b` | "spinning, powered" | no node lantern (EM-14, T); the FAULT_GLOW rim (row 9, T) |
| A's frozen pipe `pa_frozen` | "thawed" | frost sheath on the lagging (F&T row 4, T) |
| B's dead coolant leak `pb_leak` | "fine" | the greyed frost fan with no drip (T) |
| Loose parts | "a data core, treasure" | no violet and no flesh in the real sprite (ART 4.4); weight via Suit's carry cue (T, IDB) |

Rule (DA-34, VA-17): for each of the eight live faults and each authored STATE, the fault table lists its falsifier row; a fault with no truth-channel companion is a content defect unless it is the flagged exception above.

### Visual rules and the lints that enforce them

VA-1 to VA-17 are adopted in DA-39. Enforcement: VA-1 `KIT_PIECE_UNMAPPED`; VA-2 `WEAR_TOKEN_MISMATCH`; VA-3 `PALETTE_SANITY_BAND`; VA-4 and VA-5 `LOOSE_ON_TRAIL_RUN`, `FLOOR_PROP_TOO_BRIGHT`, Layout's `CLEAR_PATH_BLOCKED` (review for the plan-view check, AC-DA-107); VA-6 `VISIBLE_UNKNOWN_NO_PORT`, `VISIBLE_UNKNOWN_COMPETITOR`; VA-7 `WAKE_SINGLE_LIGHT`; VA-8 `TRAIL_NO_SOURCE`; VA-9 `HERO_CAP`; VA-10 `PIPE_BAND_BLOCKED`; VA-11 `INTRUSION_OUTSIDE_BREACH`; VA-12 `HOLE_WITHOUT_OPENING`; VA-13 review (AC-DA-53); VA-14, VA-15, VA-16 review and the Pipeline's own criteria; VA-17 `TRUTH_WITNESS_MISSING`. (orchestrator ruling: `WAKE_SINGLE_LIGHT` scopes to `cryo_bay`, the wake ROOM, per Checkpoint Q2 and ART 2.2 "it is the only fixture in this state"; lit adjoining rooms behind a closed door do not break the wake, and the lint also asserts the beacon is dark at step 0. The art-director's stricter every-room reading is not adopted.)

### Kit and asset implications

All stems follow ART 8.5, `<cat>[_<dialect>]_<subject>[_<variant tokens>]_<map>.png`, lowercase, two-digit indices; the generic naming convention is superseded as ART 8.5 explains. The manifest is DA-38 (**PROPOSED** tallies, the Foundry dialect only at MVP). Groups, stems and cells: DA-38.

**Kit gaps filed against the art bible:** K-1 to K-9: DA-38 (nothing edited here; the amendments owed to the bible are `-> OQ-V1`, deliberate amendments and not silent overrides).

**Piece-count tally (risk).** About 125 pieces against about 110 (about +14 %), mitigation order and `/asset-spec` reconciliation: DA-38, AC-DA-55. **Not in this list (owner named):** trail sprites and glow rows (F&T), beam and pool cookies (Lighting), module-body wear states (Item DB), severed stubs (Boarding & Salvage), the exterior wreck silhouette and pod (Web Map & Transit, lit only by the pod's running light), HUD marks (none), empty suits and interrupted props (Vertical Slice).

### Conflict and decision ledger (art-side)

| Id | Statement | Resolution |
|---|---|---|
| V-1 | R23 listed "scorch/soot" in the `_worn` vocabulary; scorch and soot are Graph trail vocabulary (FT-3, FT AC-44) | removed from `_worn` (Checkpoint Q3); tool scorch on a cut leaf stays exempt (VA-8) |
| V-2 | an authored open breach drawn as a static holed tile would keep showing a hole after the player plates it | the hole is drawn by the HULL_SEGMENT state (F&T row 5) plus the plate-position piece; static variants are torn or buckled with no through-hole, at most 30 % of pieces (VA-12) |
| V-3 | ART 2.2's pod interior light versus R13 | R13 accepted (none in MVP, LO:451); "warmth is spent, not owned" is carried by the guttering lamp and the 6.5-minute warm-up (D.18); ART 2.2 amendment owed (OQ-V1) |
| V-5 | the cryo bay carries a heater, an exposed duct, a leak and a drip against ART 6.4's "media 1, trails 1" | counting conventions above (periodic motion only; trails per source anchor; build state) |
| V-6 | the residual light shares a wreck with B's SCRAM rim and later lit lamps; FAULT_GLOW output follows `sev` while F&T's overlay decays with `since_steps` | the host room has no competing light (VA-6); B's SCRAM is authored with small `since_steps` (OQ-V3) |
| V-8 | the ART 6.3 MVP lists three machinery nodes; the MVP has 12 non-lamp `mod_` defs and a live fuel loop | loop-family silhouettes; gap K-8 |
| V-9 | the FAILED scrubber has no consequence-channel pixel | flagged exception, witnesses in the falsifier map; answered by Boarding & Salvage BS-17 (closed-list witnesses plus SCAN) |
| V-10 | about +14 % piece-count overrun | mitigation order above |
| V-11 | structure and props classified T before the Filter exists | `(provisional)`; re-check |
| V-12 | a dried FUEL stain could approach the flesh band | not authored at MVP; F&T's own stop (hue 20 +-3, S at least 75 %, V at most 38 %) is outside the band if ever used |
| V-13 | ambient motes could be misread as a draught | non-directional, 1 px, slow, breathable compartments only (VA-15) |

### Art-side acceptance criteria, mapped

The art-director's twelve checks are carried in Acceptance Criteria under these ids: sanity palette AC-DA-51; monochrome wreck AC-DA-52; X-1 side identification AC-DA-07; the three ART 6.0 questions AC-DA-93 (a); fault falsifier rows AC-DA-50; wake single light AC-DA-33; visible-unknown port and competitor AC-DA-37; dried-stop contrast and step AC-DA-125; wear, hole, pipe-band and source lints AC-DA-06, AC-DA-49; exterior single lit point AC-DA-38; power-restored static-then-moving pipes and the no-mask lamp AC-DA-126; piece-count reconciliation AC-DA-55.

### Audio: what the authored objects emit (citation only)

Adaptive Audio is undesigned, so this GDD authors no audio rule, layer, ducking, fade or trigger. The authored objects emit exactly what their owners' descriptors say: node lanterns hum while their machine runs (EM-14), B's SCRAM rim ticks as it decays (F&T row 9), a leak hisses with its flow, and an authored hull segment may groan before it fails (the tell is visual; whether and how it sounds is the audio-director's). The mood the visuals set for the audio-director to match: a dead wreck is still, dusty and archaeological; a woken branch is a slow rhythmic hum; the cryo bay is hushed and the drip is the loudest thing in it. Perception Filter audio lies are the Filter's and never live in an authored file.

📌 **Asset Spec** — Visual/Audio requirements are defined. After the art bible is approved, run `/asset-spec system:derelict-authoring-handmade-kits` to produce per-asset visual descriptions, dimensions and generation prompts from this section (the K-1 to K-9 pieces and the `_worn` derivation first).

## Game Feel

This system has no input and no frame of its own, so its feel is the feel of what the authored data makes happen: how soon a player's act is answered by a lamp, a pipe or a trail, how long a tell lasts before a clock ends, how far a player can run to a door, and how heavy a carry across a dead room is. Every number below is either authored here, derived from an authored value by a formula in this GDD, or cited from the owner (never restated as ours). The playtest targets at the end are `[PLACEHOLDER: creative-director]` until the first playtest.

### Input-to-feedback chain (the content-shaped acts)

| Step | What happens | Latency | Owner |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | The player holds `interact` on a breaker (V3), valve (V5), fitting (V1) or crank (V12). The acknowledgement is immediate; the channel bar fills linearly | 0 ticks to the bar; V1 `PATCH_CHANNEL_TICKS` 180 (3 s) `[PLACEHOLDER: Interaction]`; V12 `CRANK_CHANNEL_TICKS` 240 (4 s); V3 and V5 per Interaction | Interaction, Player Controller |
| 2 | The completed act calls its Graph mutator (M7, M11, M13 and the like) in the kernel's command path | applied inside the same tick it completes (kernel P-phase), never drawn before commit | Interaction, kernel |
| 3 | The Graph hook chain (`graph.propagate.power`, `.atmo`, `.failure`) runs at divisor 6, so the new `served_pm` is visible to the Emitter and the presentation at the next Graph step | at most `GRAPH_STEP_TICKS` 6 ticks = 100 ms after step 2 | Graph |
| 4 | The Emitter marks the fixture lit; Lighting warms it over `LAMP_WARM_FRAMES` (placeholder 5 frames, `[PLACEHOLDER: Lighting]`) with the prompt edge and the visible edge agreeing from the first frame (LO-19) | 5 presentation frames, about 83 ms at 60 Hz | Lighting |
| 5 | The pipe pattern on the newly served branch starts to move; frost, drip and trail respond at their own cadences (F&T) | one Graph step per hop, so a wake travels down a pipe at 10 hops per second | Pipeline, F&T |

So the answer to "I closed the breaker" is a lit room within about 12 frames of the channel completing, and the whole branch following hop by hop inside a second or two: fast enough to read as the player's cause, slow enough to watch the effect travel (P1: the pipe agrees with the gauge). What Authoring contributes to this chain is the content that makes it legible: `fx_cryo_2` is `switch_on` true behind the breaker `j_cryo` (open at wake), so the room floods with no new command (EM-13); the branch is short (the cryo branch cable `cab_cryo` is the first thing the breaker feeds); and the frost trail, the dark heater and the dim lamp are all in one frame of the wake room.

### Content timings the feel rests on (all derived, with the source)

| Quantity | Value | Derivation |
|---|---|---|
| Cryo wake: lamp gutter | 18 to 30 tick dips, no more often than every 600 to 1200 ticks | ART 2.2 as authored in E2 (Lighting's `gutter_recipe` owns the generator) |
| Warm-up of the cryo bay after V3 | the room warms at about 4 mC per Graph step gross (500 W into C = 42 x `C_TILE` 3000 = 126,000 dJ/°C, LSG D.7), about 3 mC net of hull loss at 13 exposed tiles, so about 6.5 minutes (about 3,900 Graph steps, about 23,400 ticks) from -2000 mC to COMFORT at 10000 mC; LSG:839's 2.5 minutes is its 20-tile example. The slower climb is the price of the verbatim Layout fixture (V 42, 13 exposed tiles); a second heater is the PWR-OVL trigger (DA-15 #3), so `CRYO_HEATER_COUNT` stays 1 | DA-45, D.18 warm-up line, LSG D.7 |
| Every endogenous failure's tell | 600 to 1,800 ticks (10 to 30 s), never under `W_MIN_WARNING_S` 10 s = 600 ticks | P2; the Graph's fairness gate on endogenous transitions (DA-29) |
| Longest escape route to a closable door | 1,800 px = `W_MIN_WARNING_S` x `TICK_RATE` x `V_RUN` (10 x 60 x 3): 10 s at a run, 15 s at `V_WALK` 2 | `ESCAPE_ROUTE_BUDGET_PX` (Layout D.19); authored longest is `pf_feed`, about 480 px, 160 ticks at a run (D.5) |
| One-way crossing of `a_machine` carrying a module | 7 tiles = 336 px at `V_CARRY` 1 px/tick = 336 ticks = 5.6 s | D.10: critical after 364 runs (36.4 s) at `T0`, so a crossing leaves a margin of about 31 s |
| Longest vacuum or cold round trip carrying a module | 56 tiles = 2,688 px = 2,688 ticks = 44.8 s round trip, 28 tiles each way (the lint bound; B's worst case 40 tiles = 32 s round trip) | D.7 (`TRAVERSE_TOO_LONG`) |
| Fuel Ember clock from wake | FAILED no earlier than 10 minutes (6,000 Graph steps' worth: 36,000 ticks), SCRAM recoverable | DA-16, amended R5 |
| Pump-stall tell on A (full bus, `b_a2`) | exactly 900 Graph steps = 5,400 ticks = 90 s of served pump before the stall | D.16 (zero margin; see Tuning Knobs) |
| Heat tell on B (`b_reactor`, after V12) | presentation step at `HEAT_TELL_STEP_MC` 44,700 mC 2,477 Graph steps (4.1 minutes) after V12, 293 steps (29.3 s, 1,758 ticks) before damage starts at 2,770; never under 100 steps (600 ticks) | D.18 tell lead, DA-28, AC-DA-133 `(provisional)` |
| Hull-fatigue tell on B | `seg_b_pump_n` crosses `BREACH_HP` after 30 periods (3,000 steps = 5 minutes of B-loaded time); hairline to crack to bent rib is visible for at least 10 s before the breach (P2) | D.20, D.15 |
| Patch hold before decay | `PATCH_DECAY_STEPS` 2400 = 4 minutes; a patched home leak is back at `LEAK_HP` in 4,500 steps (7.5 minutes), a derelict one in 3,500; the felt cost of this treadmill is target X-DA-9 (below), whose levers are `PATCH_DECAY_STEPS` (Graph-owned) and the home leak count | D.15, DA-25 |
| Restart race on B | `W` 90 s to run V12 after the leak is visible; V12 channel 4 s | D.21 |
| Residual lamps | A 27,000 steps = 162,000 ticks = 45 minutes loaded; B 21,600 steps = 36 minutes loaded | D.6 |
| Beacon revolution | `BEACON_SWEEP_TICKS` 128 `[PLACEHOLDER: Emitter]` = 2.13 s | Emitter registry |
| Door leaf | slides whole pixels per tick; it travels in the same tick the Graph's `area` changes (0 ticks of Layout latency) | LY-9, Layout latency row |
| Weld seam: fresh to old | `SEAM_OLD_AFTER_TICKS` 36,000 (10 minutes) | ART 6.1 |

### Weight and snap

- **Whole-pixel everything.** Leaves, plates, dust and trails move or sit on whole pixels at the integer zoom rungs; a placed prop sits on the 24 px lattice (LY-3), so a player never sees a prop one pixel off a wall. Snap target: zero sub-pixel positions in any authored record (`AUTHORED_NON_INTEGER`, `WELD_OFFSET` lattice check D.13).
- **The carry is the weight.** A module is carried at `V_CARRY` 1 (a third of a run, half a walk). The authored salvage is placed so the round trip through an unbreathable stretch to the farthest salvage and back is at most 56 tiles (44.8 s at `V_CARRY`) and the farthest hazard crossing at most 7 tiles of lethal cold (5.6 s): heavy enough to feel like a burden and a decision about the route, short enough to be fair. No authored carry route passes within 1 tile of a fault's maximum floor run.
- **Nothing decorative moves.** The only moving things in a dead wreck are the player's beam and its dust; the only sound-shaped event is a lamp, a drip or a hum that the Graph is actually producing. That stillness is the weight of a wreck.
- **No hit-stop, shake or flash is authored.** Failure is felt through the tell (a longer-lasting visible trail and a changing pipe), not through an effect on the camera.

### The three authored beats (anchor moments as feel)

1. **The cryo wake (first minute).** Slowest, quietest frame in the game: ambient about 3 %, one gutter dip every 10 to 20 s, a drip as the fastest motion, a frost line the eye follows to a fitting. The player closes `j_cryo`, the room floods warm in about 12 frames, the heater begins its 6.5-minute climb (about 4 mC per Graph step gross, D.18; the price of the verbatim Layout fixture), and the player patches the fitting in 3 s of channel. The door opens onto a lit corridor with the beacon dark; `door_engine_n` stands open and the ember glow of `pf_feed` is the next thing in view. No text, no marker (DA-45). "Warmth is spent, not owned": the flood of Tungsten is an event the player caused and paid for in power.
2. **The held-breath boarding (derelict A, first sortie).** From the pod, A is black but for one Tungsten point (the residual lamp). Arrival room: beam only, dust in the cone, a dried coolant fan at a lagged elbow. The eye follows trail, source, hatch (ART 6.5 eye line). One sweep tells the player who built it, what was done to it, and that the web has not come in. The decision beat is the dead branch: swapping the spare `mod_breaker_junction` wakes the bus, lights the pump's lantern (the FAILED scrubber's stays dark, DA-24), starts the pump-stall clock (90 s of tell) and doubles the speed of every wear row (`DERELICT_FAULT_COMPRESSION` 500). The cost lands as the player's own consequence, not an ambush.
3. **The weld seam (derelict B, then home).** B is hot and loud in the dark: a SCRAM rim glow, a draught from a vacuum room, a fatigued segment with a visible hairline-to-crack tell, a restart race with a 90 s window. After the weld the player stands at the doubled bulkhead and can say which side was the original ship without a prompt (Layout X-1, AC-DA-07).

### Playtest-checkable targets

The creative-director ruling (CD-GDD-ALIGN) endorses X-DA-1 to X-DA-7 as written and adds X-DA-8 and X-DA-9. The targets themselves are fixed; only the numeric thresholds are `[PLACEHOLDER: creative-director]` (tagged inline). Each round is **five testers, at least three of whom are outside the project**, matching the concept's kill/continue gate ("at least three people outside the project", game-concept MVP Definition). Five testers and 4-of-5 thresholds are a smoke signal, not a statistic: a second round of 8 or more is required before any threshold below is promoted from placeholder `[PLACEHOLDER: creative-director]`. Protocols and scoring sheets are in Acceptance Criteria (AC-DA-87 to AC-DA-93, AC-DA-135, AC-DA-136).

| Id | Target |
|---|---|
| X-DA-1 | At least 4 of 5 `[PLACEHOLDER: creative-director]` first-time testers name the failed loop of derelict A within 60 s of boarding, from the dried trails alone |
| X-DA-2 | At least 4 of 5 `[PLACEHOLDER: creative-director]` point at the residual light during transit and find its room aboard within 3 minutes without a marker |
| X-DA-3 | At least 3 of 5 `[PLACEHOLDER: creative-director]` pause or voice a cost before closing A's breaker or swapping its junction |
| X-DA-4 | No tester is stationary and lost for more than 30 s in the cryo bay; at least 4 of 5 `[PLACEHOLDER: creative-director]` follow the frost trail to its fitting unprompted |
| X-DA-5 | Layout X-1 as written (seam-side identification), cited not restated; AC-DA-07 and AC-DA-91 |
| X-DA-6 | At least 3 of 5 `[PLACEHOLDER: creative-director]` testers, asked "could you have taken more?", answer about choice or risk, not an inventory display |
| X-DA-7 | The door state (open or closed) is identified correctly from three tiles in greyscale by at least 4 of 5 `[PLACEHOLDER: creative-director]` reviewers |
| X-DA-8 | Restraint is a real strategy (P3): across 5 testers both the wake-the-branch and the leave-it-dark outcome on A occur at least once, and every tester who left it dark returned home having taken the cut by beam, and can say why they carried it or left it |
| X-DA-9 | The patch treadmill is not a chore (P5 against the MVP core hypothesis): fewer than 2 of 5 `[PLACEHOLDER: creative-director]` testers describe re-patching the home leaks as tedious or a chore in the first 45 minutes. Levers if it fails: `PATCH_DECAY_STEPS` (Graph-owned, a routed ask) and the home leak count (3); the ledger is not changed before the playtest (DA-25) |

## UI Requirements

**Derelict Authoring has no UI of its own; every surface that shows an authored object is owned by another GDD.** It authors files, not a screen: no HUD element, instrument, menu, prompt, marker, map overlay or notification is named or implied here, and none may be inferred from a field in a template. This is a design decision (P1, P3, the anti-pillars and DA-37), not an omission: the world is the display, so a wreck's richness, danger and history are shown by a lit room, a dried trail, a pipe that agrees with its gauge and one warm point in the dark, never by a rating, a "salvage value", an objective marker, a minimap pin, a "cleared" tick, an icon on the visible unknown or a danger meter from a fault rate.

What touches a player-facing surface, and who owns it:

| Authored fact | Shown by | Owner |
|---|---|---|
| Lamps, residual light, beacon, glowsticks, lanterns | the world, as light | Emitter State, Lighting |
| Faults, dried trails, frost, hole, airflow | the world, as trails and glow rows | Failure & Trail |
| Module `hp` and condition, loose items | the sprite and the interact prompt | Item DB, Interaction |
| Door and hatch state | the leaf sprite | Layout (PROJECTOR) |
| Gauges and instruments reading authored systems | the in-world instruments and the inspect card, corruptible and falsifiable | In-World Instruments & Inspect View (K-4); placement is Authoring's (DA-34, IW-31) |
| The exterior view of a wreck and its `visible_unknown` | the transit view | Web Map & Transit (undesigned) |
| Save, death restart | the pause panel and the failure stub | Save/Load |
| The content lint report | **plain text in the developer console and CI log**; not a player surface and not part of a release build's UI | Authoring (DA-40, DA-41) |

There is therefore no UX Flag for this system and no `/ux-design` pass is required for it. Two constraints are handed to the owners above, both as content rules this GDD enforces by lint: in MVP no authored piece, template or lint message may carry text, a label, an icon or an arrow (VA-13, AC-DA-53; VA-13 targets UI-like markers, not future diegetic lore fragments, which are the narrative-director's), and no authored record may carry a field whose only use is to be drawn on a HUD. A UI that wants to display more about a wreck has to find it in the world first (P1).

#### 2. CONFLICT paragraphs and routed wording fixes (nothing below edits another GDD)

- **CONFLICT C-1, the weld donor (LSG vs LY).** LY-13 ("exactly one socket on the home-ship ... and its mate on derelict B"), Layout's `fixture_derelict_b_socket` and IA D.10 ("home ship plus derelict B loaded", the weld case) all name derelict **B** as the donor; one Graph acceptance clause (LSG:1133) says "the section salvaged from derelict A" while its own parenthesis ("D.16's 35/45/5 cost row is the derelict-B worst case") already assumes B. Adopted: **A yields a module, B yields the welded section** (DA-2). The defective clause is not quoted as a contract and no value changes. Route to the Graph owner: replace "derelict A" with "derelict B" in that criterion.
- **CONFLICT C-2, "spawn".** LSG:228 tags derelict A "(cold wreck, spawn)" and LY:162 reads "the spawn room for derelict A", while the cryo bay and the wake are in the home-ship (LSG:228, LSG:839). Adopted: the home-ship is the spawn derelict; `entry_room_key` has **one** meaning, the room the pod arrives in (DA-6). Route one-line corrections: LSG:228 "cold wreck, spawn" to "cold wreck, first sortie"; LY:162 "the spawn room for derelict A" to "the pod-arrival room for derelict A".
- **CONFLICT C-3 (closed 2026-10-27):** the registry unit string was corrected in the consistency pass and no longer carries 'once powered'; no wording note is owed.
- **CONFLICT C-4, hardpoints (resolved 2026-10-27).** `home_ship` needs 27 hardpoints (26 module nodes including the beacon lamp `bcn_main` + the vacant `hp_cargo_heater`; world 67); the registry default `MAX_HARDPOINTS_PER_SECTION` was raised from 24 to 32 (range 12 to 64), so no waiver or removal is needed. Route 2 (remove three home vents) is not taken.
- **CONFLICT C-5, wear vocabulary (ruling R23 vs FT-3 / FT AC-44).** R23 listed "scorch/soot" in `_worn`; adopted narrowed vocabulary (DA-5). Route to the art-director: the bible's 6.2 weathering vocabulary lists scorch as a *Graph-driven trail*; no kit tile carries it.
- **CONFLICT C-6, fault staging (ruling R5 vs Graph arithmetic).** Adopted O1 with the witness condition: the amended R5, the options considered and the Graph arithmetic are stated once in DA-16; R5's literal "rho 1100" for PWR-OVL is met at its trigger, not at the first V3 (DA-15 #3, D.9).
- **CONFLICT C-7, the residual charge (ruling R7).** R7's worked 25 % / 75 min outlives two sessions against its own "not two" and ignores LS-17; adopted 15 % / 12 % on loaded time (DA-27).
- **CONFLICT C-8, door leaf states (ruling R18 and R23 (iv)).** Adopted closed and open only on interior doors (DA-8).
- **Ship Body CONFLICT row 26 (seat 0) resolved:** Authoring renumbers the spawn table to seat 1 (`spawn_cryo_1`, `seat_pod_1`); seat 0 is Body's "no seat" (BDY-7) and PlayerIds start at 1 (KRN) (DA-3). Attention's refresh C20 ("may relocate it") is absorbed in DA-24: Attention only submits `set_beacon` to the one beacon the data holds (AT-18).
- **Beacon housing stem.** Attention's asset row A6 names the beacon housing `env_shared_beacon_housing`; DA-38 adopts that stem (the beacon is a shared piece) in place of `fixture_housing_beacon`. No Attention edit is owed; the art-director confirms the `env_shared_` prefix against ART 8.5 (OQ-V1).
- **Warm-up figure (Graph owner).** LSG:839's "2.5 minutes" to COMFORT is the Graph's 20-tile example; the authored 42-tile cryo bay warms in about 6.5 minutes (D.18). Route a one-line note to the Graph owner that the figure assumes V 20; no value changes.
- **Layout's fixture lanes are unchanged.** `fixture_airlock` and `fixture_derelict_b_socket` carry COOLANT + POWER lanes as "fixture content, not a design decision" (LY:1258); this GDD's sockets carry POWER + O2 + hatch (DA-10). They are schema examples and no conflict is to be filed. Likewise `fixture_airlock` (2 x 2) cannot satisfy LY D.18's 240 px hatch run; the authored airlock is 5 tiles tall. The authored `sock_b_w` also differs from `fixture_derelict_b_socket` in position (`line_px` 24, `start_px` 288 vs 120, 144), hatch id (`door_b_hatch_w` vs `door_b_w`) and `area_max` (64 vs 128); the fixture is a schema example (LY:1249) and no conflict is to be filed.
- **CONFLICT C-9, Lighting's C3 interim rule (answered 2026-10-27).** Lighting rewrote C3 on 2026-10-27 to a per-face spill depth s = max(0, r_draw - d_face - WALL_FOOTPRINT_DEPTH) (hull walls exempt); the 244 px reading is withdrawn. Under it 2 of 14 rooms are PD-1 exceptions (DA-31). `CONTAIN_COLOURS` 4 is sufficient (3 needed).
- **CONFLICT C-10, the interaction subject cap.** The post-weld sortie set S4 needs 144 against `INTERACT_FIXTURE_BUDGET` 120 `[PLACEHOLDER: Interaction]` (DA-32); ask Interaction (ASK-I1, OQ-DA-20) and, for the budget route, Interaction and the Emitter jointly (144 with `MAX_EXPOSURE_SUBJECTS` 224).
- **Concept and index wording.** CON MVP item 1 ("one handmade spawn derelict and one handmade target derelict, with an abstracted pod hop") and IDX row 14 / its rationale bullet ("two handmade derelicts") are summaries of three sections and two hops; route a wording refresh (not edited here).
- **Art bible amendments owed (art-director, OQ-V1).** ART 2.2 (the pod interior light, dropped by R13), ART 6.3 (machinery-node note, wall port, pod prop, cap plate, dock fitting; the "breached variants ... holed state" clause, narrowed so a through-hole is never a static tile), ART 6.4 ("permanent dead-ship geometry" for a Graph-live breach), ART 8.2 (a 96 x 96 prop cell), ART 8.5 (`worn`, `smashed` tokens).
- **Count notes.** LS-26's "about 40 / 30 / 35 nodes, about 55 / 45 edges, about 8 LOADs" are targets; this GDD's counts are 42 / 27 / 35 nodes, 34 / 21 / 29 design edges and 11 home LOADs (over target by 3, equal to the tolerance, so passing with no margin), all passing the one-sided tolerance (D.2).
- **CONFLICT C-11, locus-body teeth (Attention AC-AT-117 / C26 vs EM-24 and LS-26).** Attention requires every possible locus body on A and B to hold a subject authored ACTIVE in OVERLOAD or `fatigued`. Derelict A alone holds none: `j_a_main` is a dead SHORT at `intensity_pm` 0, `pump_a` is STARVE and `pa_frozen` is forced FREEZE. Adding one would be a ninth live fault (EM-24 "exactly 8") and a lethal clock on the cold wreck. B passes (`seg_b_pump_n` 6999 `fatigued`). Routed to Attention: amend AC-AT-117 so that a locus body passes if it holds a fault that wakes when fed (A: `j_a_main` once the bus is served), or exempt a dead-bus derelict as the home-ship is exempt. No value changes here.
- **Interaction's dead-reactor beat (IA Player Fantasy, IA:54).** IA describes B's first V12 returning `BLOCKED(STILL_HOT)`. As authored, `r_b` is cold enough at build that the first V12 succeeds (DA-35, D.21), because the restart chain is B's must-prove. The beat survives as the re-SCRAM case: if the player skips the `pb_leak` patch, `r_b` re-SCRAMs within 90 s with `T_mach` above `OVERHEAT_OFF_MC`, and an immediate second V12 returns STILL_HOT. Routed to Interaction as a wording note (the beat is texture, no value changes).
- **CONFLICT C-12, `N_GLOW_DARK` (Attention F-16).** Attention reads 2 glows active at wake citing DA-16; `N_GLOW_DARK` is 1 under the closed-valve staging (FUEL-DRIP `1/FUEL`) and 2 under the fallback that ships until ASK-G1 is accepted (adding O2-SHIP `1/GAS`, DA-16's trigger), so Attention's 2 is the shipping value today. Routed to Attention; the value follows OQ-DA-18.
- **Routed ask ASK-F&T-H1 (Failure & Trail and 2D Lighting; CD-GDD-ALIGN concern, applied; nothing here edits either GDD).** `b_reactor`'s HOT shimmer is a standing tell from `T0` 42000 mC, so without a change it looks the same when the room crosses `HEAT_DMG_ONSET_MC` 45000 and starts to damage (P2: nothing escalates). Ask: provide one visible *step* in the room-level heat presentation (a band change in shimmer amplitude or rate; a hue step is allowed only inside the sane palette and never into the flesh band, hue 5 to 30) at `HEAT_TELL_STEP_MC` (Authoring request, 44,700 mC as authored, range 44,000 to 44,800), so the step lands at least `W_MIN_WARNING_S` x 10 = 100 Graph steps before `t_onset_steps` (D.18; 293 steps as authored). F&T's heat shimmer today is a sev-2 stepped strip at the fitting (FT:942, FT:1087, FT-24) and Lighting's heat read has no onset band; which of the two owns the step is theirs to answer (OQ-DA-37). The fix is not a marker: "told by the shimmer, never by a marker" (DA-28) is unchanged.

## Acceptance Criteria

*Every criterion is pass/fail from this section plus the glossary below. **Tags:** **(auto)** a headless test under `tests/integration/derelict_authoring/` or `tests/unit/derelict_authoring/` using the kernel's headless path (H10, no scene tree, no window); **(lint)** the CONTENT lint of DA-40, offline and in CI (BOOT rules are marked); **(review)** a code or document review with a retained record (reviewer, date, hits found); **(manual)** an instrumented play or lit capture with retained evidence in `production/qa/evidence/derelict-authoring/`; **(spike)** a pass/fail number from a time-boxed ENGINE spike. Modifiers: **(ASSET)** run by `tools/asset-pipeline/` over sheets, not templates; **(perf)** a timing measurement; **(integration)** needs more than one system running. Bracketed ids name the Core Rule (`DA-n`), Formula (`D.n`), edge case or owner criterion a line discharges. Playtest numbers marked `[PLACEHOLDER: creative-director]` stay placeholders until a second round of 8 or more testers. **`tests/` and the gdUnit4 addon do not exist yet (Item DB OQ 28): every (auto) and (lint) line is a specification until the project's test-setup story lands, and none may be reported as passed before then.** Criteria blocked on an open ask say BLOCKED-ON and cite it; a blocked criterion is never marked passed by this GDD. Story-type mapping: lint lines are Config/Data (treated as BLOCKING in CI regardless of `testing.strict.config`, because DA-41 makes every ERROR a merge block); lines needing the Graph to run are Integration (BLOCKING); captures are Visual/Feel (BLOCKING, retained screenshot plus lead sign-off, not waived at `qa.level: minimal`).*

### Glossary of names used below

| Name | Meaning |
|---|---|
| V1, V3, V5, V8, V9, V12 | verbs: V1 patch a fitting; V3 close a breaker (restore power); V5 open or close a valve; V8 cut or salvage a module; V9 weld; V12 hand-crank a SCRAMmed reactor (M11) |
| fault ids | the eight live faults by `fault_id`: `CLT-LEAK` (`p_cryo_rad`), `O2-SHIP` (`d_cryo_exposed`), `PWR-OVL` (`cab_trunk`), `FUEL-DRIP` (`pf_feed`) on `home_ship`; broken breaker (`j_a_main`) and pump stall (`pump_a`) on A; `O2-ROOM` (the weld seam) and `HULL-FATIGUE` (`seg_b_pump_n`) on B (DA-15) |
| literal / gated reading | of Graph D.9: *literal* = a leak-mode subject loses `hp` while in mode LEAK even at `intensity_pm` 0; *gated* = wear only while `intensity_pm` > 0 (ASK-G1) |
| LSG D.15 / D.15 | "LSG D.15" is the Graph's fault-fixture table; "D.15" alone is this GDD's patch and fatigue treadmill |
| ASK-G1 | ask to the Graph owner: gate leak wear on `intensity_pm` > 0 (**BLOCKING** for the closed-valve staging; OQ-DA-18) |
| ASK-G3 | ask to the Graph and Item DB owners: the scrubber's `R_SCR` is 200 in one place and 400 in another |
| ASK-I1 | ask to Interaction: register subjects by body adjacency or proximity, not by "loaded" (OQ-DA-20) |
| OQ-DA-n, OQ-V*n* | Open Questions rows of this GDD (the table at the end); OQ-V rows are the art-director's |
| `S0` to `S4` | the five reachable loaded sets: S0 `{home}`, S1 `{home, A}`, S2 `{home, B}`, S3 `{home+B}`, S4 `{home+B, A}` (DA-32) |
| art-side checks | the art-director's twelve are carried as AC-DA-07, 33, 37, 38, 49, 50, 51, 52, 55, 93, 125 and 126 (see Visual/Audio Requirements, last sub-sections) |

- **AC-DA-01 (review)** GIVEN the Authoring source tree and the kernel registries, WHEN scanned, THEN Authoring registers no state block (CR-8), no step hook (CR-3), no mutator and no `read_view`, and no Authoring file imports a scene-tree class. [DA-1; kernel H10]
- **AC-DA-02 (lint)** GIVEN `assets/data/world/mvp_world.json`, WHEN read, THEN it lists exactly `home_ship`, `derelict_a`, `derelict_b` and the `pod` stub (kind as Layout decides, OQ-DA-17), each `dialect` `foundry`, `rot_q` 0, `SECTION_SCHEMA_VERSION` 1; `entry_room_key` is `airlock`, `a_dock` and `b_dock` respectively; only `home_ship` has `kind` HOME; and no `template_id`, `room_key` or `entry_room_key` contains the string `spawn`. [DA-2, DA-6]
- **AC-DA-03 (lint, auto)** GIVEN a Body `can_place` stub that applies Layout's rect set and the five spawn rows `spawn_cryo_1`, `arrive_home_airlock`, `arrive_a_dock`, `arrive_b_dock`, `seat_pod_1`, WHEN `SPAWN_BLOCKED` runs, THEN each row passes `BDY_PLACE_INSIDE_FOOTPRINT`, its room is breathable at step 0, none lies within 1 tile of a fault's maximum floor run, and each arrival cell is 1 tile clear of the docked-podcraft prop. [DA-3; BDY-20, BDY-25]
- **AC-DA-04 (lint)** GIVEN every authored number in the four templates and the world file, WHEN `AUTHORED_NON_INTEGER` runs, THEN zero findings; every battery value is an integer dJ and no field named `percent` or `pct` exists. [DA-4]
- **AC-DA-05 (review)** GIVEN the DA-4 mapping table and the templates, WHEN every added content field is traced, THEN each maps one-to-one onto an owner field (BATTERY `stock`, VALVE `open`, O2_TANK `stock`, RESERVOIR and FUEL_TANK `stock_ml`, COMPARTMENT `T`, `breaker_closed`, `hp`, `forced_mode`, `fail_since_step`, FIXTURE `switch_on`, the loose item's `condition_bp`) and no added field is without an owner. [DA-4; guard rail G1]
- **AC-DA-06 (lint)** GIVEN the three templates, WHEN `WEAR_TOKEN_MISMATCH` runs, THEN `home_ship` references only maintained pieces and both wrecks only `_worn` pieces, and no `_worn` piece contains scorch, soot, char, rust, a warm stain or a fluid stain (the closed vocabulary of DA-5). [DA-5]
- **AC-DA-07 (manual)** GIVEN one lit capture at the doubled bulkhead after the weld with the same beam on both sides, WHEN five blind reviewers are asked which side is the original ship, THEN at least 4 of 5 name the maintained side; if the art spike cannot meet it, X-1 is downgraded to Vertical Slice in Layout's text. `[PLACEHOLDER: creative-director]` [DA-5; Layout X-1]

Since the CD-GDD-ALIGN pass the build also protects P2 at the fairness floor: `PUMP_TELL_UNDER_FAIRNESS` (AC-DA-134) ERRORs below 100 steps even if the 900-step target slips, so drift is caught at the lint and not discovered in play. [D.16]
- **AC-DA-134 (lint)** GIVEN derelict A's bus with every served request counted at its conservative value (a FAILED `scr_a` still requests 600 W, the `[UNV: Graph]` assumption of D.16), WHEN `PUMP_TELL_UNDER_FAIRNESS` runs, THEN `t_bus_steps` = floor(`C_bus` / `R_served`) is at least `W_MIN_WARNING_S` x `TICK_RATE` / 6 = 100 steps on every bus configuration (full bus, `scr_a` cut, pump alone) or the build ERRORs, and the lint output prints the intended `PUMP_TELL_MIN_STEPS` 900 (90 s) design target beside the computed figure, with a `PUMP_TELL_BELOW_TARGET` WARN when the figure is under the target; and GIVEN `b_a2` lowered to 1,440,000 dJ x 0.1 (144,000 dJ) or a pump draw raised until `t_bus_steps` is 99, THEN the ERROR fires although no authored file changed. [D.16, DA-29; P2]
- **AC-DA-78 (lint)** GIVEN D.17, WHEN the I-7 relation is evaluated for budgets 120 and 144, THEN 120 passes and 144 fails unless `MAX_EXPOSURE_SUBJECTS` `[PLACEHOLDER: Emitter]` is at least 224. [D.17]
- **AC-DA-79 (lint)** GIVEN D.18 with the fixed-point table (so 26.9 and 24.3 are byte-stable across machines), WHEN `a_dock` `T0` is set to -6000, THEN `t_cross_min` is 26.9 and `COLD_ROOM_DRIFT` is silent; at -8000 it is 24.3 and the WARN fires. [D.18]
- **AC-DA-80 (auto)** GIVEN D.19 and the shipped staging named in the run header (DA-16's trigger), WHEN one crew is sealed in `cryo_bay` with `v_o2_cryo` closed (the authored state under the closed-valve staging; under the valve-OPEN fallback the harness closes it before the run), THEN the O2 band reaches THIN at 4,200 steps (tolerance 1); with the valve open and the scrubber unserved the pool is empty at 5,455 +-818 (the unserved branch assumes `R_SCR` 0). The served branch (8,572 steps at `R_SCR` 200, 20,000 at 400) is BLOCKED-ON ASK-G3. [D.19]
- **AC-DA-81 (auto)** GIVEN D.20, WHEN B idles loaded, THEN `seg_b_pump_n` is at 3,999 after 30 periods and DECOMP is reached at period 40 +-1. [D.20]

### Cross-system criteria (owner echoes)

- **AC-DA-82 (auto)** GIVEN the MVP home-ship template, WHEN loaded, THEN it has 5 compartments (`cryo_bay`, `spine_corridor`, `cargo_bay`, `engine_room`, `airlock`), one HEATER, one exposed DUCT in the cryo bay, 7 HULL_SEGMENTs, 42 nodes and 34 design edges (the Graph's "about 40" and "about 55" read as estimates; the per-loop islands are power 17 nodes with 16 CABLE edges, O2 7, coolant 5, fuel 2, OPENING 4), the cryo bay at `T` -2000 mC with a coolant PIPE in LEAK, and the first run emits a LEAK/COOLANT descriptor anchored in the cryo bay with `ATMO_COLD` sev 2 (cited from the Graph's MVP-content criterion). [LSG MVP-content criteria; OQ-DA-22]
- **AC-DA-83 (manual)** GIVEN the MVP weld on a dev build, WHEN played, THEN a capture shows the seam weeping from the weld, no change in the home-ship gauges until the hatch is opened, and the draught between the rooms when it is, with the section donated by **derelict B** (the wording fix to LSG:1133 is routed); captures retained. `(Modular Ship Assembly is on disk, In Design; the harness drives Layout's `apply` until its build exists)` [LSG MVP-content criteria; C-1]
- **AC-DA-84 (auto)** GIVEN the six MVP hazard modes and the recipe LEAK (`p_cryo_rad`, idle), SHORT/OVERLOAD (`cab_trunk`, heater installed and `j_cryo` closed), OVERHEAT/SCRAM (`r_main` after `pf_feed` FAILED), FREEZE (`pa_frozen`, forced), BREACH (`seg_b_pump_n` after 30 periods), STARVE (`pump_a` once the bus is served), WHEN the fixtures run, THEN each mode appears on at least one descriptor with `sev` >= 1, read from the descriptor list, not the render (STARVE draws nothing, F&T row 6). [LSG MVP-content criteria]
- **AC-DA-85 (auto)** GIVEN the MVP slice played headless with the welded body, WHEN the lantern, fault-glow, non-shadow and shadow counts are read, THEN they stay within `LANTERN_LIGHTS_MAX` `[PLACEHOLDER: Lighting]`, `NONSHADOW_LIGHTS_MAX` `[PLACEHOLDER: Lighting]` and `SHADOW_SLOTS_MAX` `[PLACEHOLDER: Lighting]`, and glow rows in use never exceed `glow_bound` 7 (common case, 5 hops) or 8 (after V12 wakes B's dead coolant fault, 6 hops), both at most `MAX_FAULT_GLOW` `[PLACEHOLDER: Emitter]`, or the demotion path runs with no simulation report. [AC-LO-60; D.12]
- **AC-DA-86 (review)** GIVEN the GDDs that name Authoring as an owner, WHEN each "provisional, Derelict Authoring undesigned" clause is read, THEN it points at the DA rule that answers it (the Cross-References table), and the routed wording fixes (C-1, C-2, C-3, the concept and index summaries, the art bible amendments) are filed with their owners, none applied by this GDD; the review record names reviewer, date and hits. [Cross-References]

### Experience targets (playtest-checkable)

Each round is five testers, at least three of whom are outside the project (the concept's kill/continue gate). The targets are endorsed (creative-director ruling); only the thresholds are `[PLACEHOLDER: creative-director]`. Five testers and 4-of-5 thresholds are a smoke signal: a second round of 8 or more is required before any threshold is promoted `[PLACEHOLDER: creative-director]`. Each run keeps its scoring sheet, observer log and sign-off in `production/qa/evidence/derelict-authoring/`.

- **AC-DA-87 (manual)** X-DA-1: GIVEN five first-time testers boarding derelict A with a stopwatch started when `a_dock` loads, WHEN asked within 60 s, THEN at least 4 of 5 name the failed loop; counted correct if the tester says cold, frozen or coolant, or electrical, power or short (either dried trail).
- **AC-DA-88 (manual)** X-DA-2: GIVEN five testers in transit, WHEN the wreck appears, THEN at least 4 of 5 point at the residual light, and at least 4 of 5 are standing in the lamp's host room (`a_life` on A, `b_breach` on B) within 3 minutes of boarding without a marker.
- **AC-DA-89 (manual)** X-DA-3: GIVEN five testers at A's dead branch, WHEN they reach the breaker decision, THEN at least 3 of 5 pause (no movement input for at least 3 s with the breaker in reach) or voice a cost; the observer records which.
- **AC-DA-90 (manual)** X-DA-4: GIVEN five testers in the cryo bay, WHEN observed, THEN none is lost (no movement input and no interaction) for more than 30 s and at least 4 of 5 enter the trail-to-fitting cells before any other exit. BLOCKED-ON OQ-DA-1 for the far-wall frost (`p_cryo_rad` `fail_since_step` -60, DA-15 #1).
- **AC-DA-91 (manual)** X-DA-5: GIVEN a tester after one weld and one session, WHEN asked to walk to the seam and say which side was the original ship without a prompt, THEN they name the maintained side (Layout X-1; the blind-capture form is AC-DA-07).
- **AC-DA-92 (manual)** X-DA-6: GIVEN five testers after a session, WHEN asked "could you have taken more?", THEN at least 3 of 5 answer about choice or risk, classified by a rubric (choice and risk vocabulary versus inventory vocabulary) by two raters with a third resolving disagreements.
- **AC-DA-93 (manual)** GIVEN five testers who entered derelict A, WHEN scored, THEN (a) each names after leaving the loop that failed (coolant or power) and the cold, scored against the fault table, and answers the three ART 6.0 questions from the world alone (who built it, what was done to it, has the web come in), at least 4 of 5; (b) a still of each door at three tiles in greyscale, open versus closed, is identified correctly by at least 4 of 5 (X-DA-7); and (c) asked afterwards whether the lamp coming on at home was something they did or something that happened, at least 4 of 5 say they did it. [Game Feel; Visual/Audio]
- **AC-DA-135 (manual)** X-DA-8: GIVEN five testers (at least three outside the project) who each reach A's dead branch, WHEN the observer logs each tester's outcome from the world state (the branch's lanterns lit and `j_a_main` swapped = wake; the branch still dark with the failed `scr_a` cut by beam alone = leave-it-dark), THEN across the five both outcomes occur at least once, and every tester logged leave-it-dark took the cut by beam and, asked at the debrief, can say why they carried the scrubber home or left it; if only one outcome occurs in five, the result is "restraint is not reading as a real choice" and is reported to the creative-director, not averaged away. [P3; Player Fantasy anchor moment]
- **AC-DA-136 (manual)** X-DA-9: GIVEN five testers (at least three outside the project) playing the first 45 minutes of the MVP without being told the treadmill is a tuning concern, WHEN each is asked, unprompted by wording that names it, how re-patching the home leaks felt, THEN fewer than 2 of 5 `[PLACEHOLDER: creative-director]` describe it as tedious or a chore (classified by the AC-DA-92 two-rater rubric, a third resolving disagreement); on failure the report names `PATCH_DECAY_STEPS` and the home leak count as the levers, and the part ledger (714 permille) is left unchanged until the next round. [DA-25, D.15; MVP core hypothesis]

### Performance and budgets

- **AC-DA-94 (lint)** GIVEN the four templates, WHEN loaded, THEN authored nodes are at most 120, emitters per reachable set are at most `MAX_EMITTERS` 96 `[PLACEHOLDER: Emitter]` (authored S4: 40), subjects per set are within D.17, and each template file is at most 256 KB `[PLACEHOLDER: producer]`.
- **AC-DA-95 (spike, perf)** GIVEN the BOOT subset (six rules) over the four templates on the reference machine named in `project.yaml` `performance` (`[PLACEHOLDER: technical-director]` until set), WHEN timed on a Release export, THEN it completes in at most 100 ms `[PLACEHOLDER: producer]` and the full CONTENT lint in CI completes in at most 60 s; neither runs in the simulation tick, so `STEP_BUDGET_MS` 4 is not touched by the lint.
- **AC-DA-96 (auto)** GIVEN the content acceptance suite (AC-DA-03, 11, 15, 16, 17, 21, 22, 23, 24, 28, 29, 33, 34, 36, 41, 42, 48, 57, 67, 71, 75, 76, 77, 80, 81, 82, 84, 85, 97, 98, 99, 100, 102, 108, 111, 112, 113, 114, 118, 120, 124), WHEN run headless in CI, THEN the suite completes in at most 5 minutes `[PLACEHOLDER: producer]` and gdUnit4 reports zero orphan nodes and zero leaked `RefCounted` handles.
- **AC-DA-121 (auto, perf)** GIVEN S4 `{home+B, A}` loaded and idle with all eight faults active, WHEN 600 consecutive Graph hook runs, Emitter updates and Interaction `sync_section` calls are timed headless on the reference machine, THEN the 99th-percentile simulation time per tick is at most `STEP_BUDGET_MS` 4 `[PLACEHOLDER: technical-director]` with no over-budget log line, and the same run on S0 is printed so the delta is visible. The content loads 104 nodes, up to 21 fixtures, 8 lanterns, 1 beacon, 10 glow rows and 144 interaction subjects every tick, so it does touch the budget. [DA-32; LSG D.16; kernel `STEP_BUDGET_MS`]
- **AC-DA-122 (spike, perf)** GIVEN the Release export on the reference machine, WHEN the world is built (`init_world` stub, three `build_section`, M1, E1, `place_loose`), THEN world-build time is at most `[PLACEHOLDER: producer]` ms, S4 frame time at the maximum emitter set (21 fixtures, 8 lanterns, 1 beacon, glow bound 8, 6 shadow slots) is at most 16.6 ms at the 95th percentile on minimum spec (the frame budget is the Pipeline's and Lighting's; cited, not redefined), and kit atlas VRAM (about 125 pieces, `_worn` doubling) is reported against a budget the technical-director sets.

### Gaps / untestable as written

The criteria above are complete as specifications; the following cannot be marked passed by this GDD, and the list is the QA position of record.

- **BLOCKED-ON owners:** AC-DA-19, AC-DA-99, AC-DA-59 and AC-DA-90 (the sign of `fail_since_step`, OQ-DA-1; the last two for the far-wall frost); AC-DA-21, AC-DA-22, AC-DA-24, AC-DA-75, AC-DA-124 (ASK-G1); AC-DA-33 and AC-DA-34 (Emitter placeholders); AC-DA-41 (Graph owner confirms the `a_machine` equilibrium); AC-DA-42 (Suit placeholders); AC-DA-46 (Lighting's `CONTAIN_COLOURS`); AC-DA-47 (ASK-I1 or the joint ask); AC-DA-80 (ASK-G3); AC-DA-114 (OQ-DA-11); AC-DA-115 (OQ-DA-17); AC-DA-116 (OQ-DA-19); AC-DA-130 (OQ-DA-30); AC-DA-132 (OQ-DA-34); AC-DA-98 and AC-DA-99 (`init_world` and the Save/Load harness).
- **Depend on unbuilt or undesigned systems:** AC-DA-11, AC-DA-15 (O2-ROOM seam instance), AC-DA-23, AC-DA-83 and AC-DA-85 need a weld and Modular Ship Assembly (on disk, In Design) has no build yet, so the harness drives the Graph's `weld_join` and Layout's LM2 `attach_section` directly (standing in for Assembly's atomic apply) with a stated weld quality `q`; AC-DA-38 needs Web Map & Transit and the Perception Filter.
- **Release-blocking position (qa-lead):** AC-DA-22 is release-blocking for MVP. The closed-valve staging ships only if the Graph owner accepts ASK-G1 before MVP content lock; if ASK-G1 is declined or still unanswered at content lock, the valve-OPEN fallback (`v_o2_cryo` OPEN at wake) ships (DA-16).
- **Zero margin:** AC-DA-77 passes at exactly 900 steps with no margin against the 90 s design target; one step of drift in a `[UNV: Graph]` assumption fails that target (recovery in the criterion). The fairness floor is separate and guarded by the build: `PUMP_TELL_UNDER_FAIRNESS` (AC-DA-134) ERRORs below 100 steps, so P2 does not depend on the arithmetic.
- **Playtest numbers** (AC-DA-59, 87 to 93) are smoke signals on five testers and stay `[PLACEHOLDER: creative-director]` until a round of 8 or more.
- **Not testable by this GDD at all:** the Perception Filter's behaviour on authored content (undesigned); whether the engine's JSON loader, export filter and editor tooling behave as assumed (`[UNV: engine]`, AC-DA-60); the frame budget (the Pipeline's and Lighting's).
- **Naming and layout of the test files** follow `.claude/rules/test-standards.md`, which this GDD did not re-read; the test-setup story confirms the file names.

## Open Questions

Owner is `user` (a scope, fiction or sign-off decision only the user can make), `agent` (a named specialist or the owner GDD's author can answer) or `GDD` (this document resolves it on the next revision). Target is `playtest`, `next GDD` (the owner GDD's next revision or a downstream GDD) or `ADR`. Rows cited in the body carry their id there; the rest are routed asks. **BLOCKING** means an MVP acceptance criterion cannot pass while it is open. User-decision items come first.

The literal alternative is a standing rho of 1.106 from the first V3, which FAILS the trunk 2.9 minutes into the warm-up (DESTROYED at 4.2). **Closed in this run (kept for the record).** OQ-DA-3, R7's charge arithmetic: replaced by loaded-time clocks (15 % and 12 % of `BATT_CELL`; A 45 and B 36 minutes) and accepted at the advisor checkpoint. OQ-DA-9, may the beacon share a node with a fixture: **no**, `bcn_main` is a dedicated LOAD node, because `set_beacon` off issues `set_load_request(node_ref, 0)` and would zero a shared lamp while its light stayed lit (P1). The FUEL-DRIP lever and witness (Checkpoint Q1), the wake-light scope (Q2), the `_worn` vocabulary (Q3), the pod-stub ownership split (Q6), `COLD_ROOM_SAFE_MIN` 25 (Q7) and `pb_leak` 2900 (Q8) were ruled at the checkpoint and are in the body.
